    11	## 一、问题（状态 A，全部可复现）
    12	
    13	本 change 打包三件互相独立、但共同指向「**默认值与语法把合法输入判成了缺陷**」的事。
    14	
    15	### 1.1 默认 id-pattern 认不出真实项目的 ID（复盘 FP-1）
    16	
    17	`lib/config.js:16`：
    18	
    19	```js
    20	const DEFAULT_ID = '[A-Z]+-\\d+';
    21	```
    22	
    23	它认不出**三段式**（`AC-BIS-01`、`LIFE-DWS-01`）与**带字母后缀**（`AC-30f`）的 ID。
    24	这类 ID 完全符合「可绑定 ID」的实质要求——唯一、稳定、可被测试名携带——只是不匹配这条正则。
    25	
    26	**为什么后缀也认不出**：`leadId`（`lib/spec-runner.js`）在正则匹配之后还查边界——
    27	
    28	```js
    29	const next = text.trim()[m[0].length];
    30	if (next !== undefined && /[A-Za-z0-9_]/.test(next)) return null;
    31	```
    32	
    33	所以 `AC-30f` 里即使 `AC-30` 匹配上，紧跟的 `f` 也会让它作废。
    34	
    35	**影响链**：verify 报 GAPS → gate C1 BLOCKED → 每个 change 都要在 flow-state 里手写一段
    36	「这是结构局限不是缺陷」的免责说明。
    37	
    38	**本次实测（棕地样本 `/mnt/d/agent-base/t_just-projects/apriori/specs`）**：
    39	共 **151** 个场景，按现行默认式有 **50** 个绑不上；换成 §二 提议的默认式后 **0** 个绑不上。
    40	
    41	**为什么 4.1.0 的修复没解决它**：`gate-id-pattern` 那个 change 把 id-pattern 变成了**项目可配置**，
    42	但**默认值一个字没动**，而配置行住在 `apriori/process-config.md`——一个 **R3 人类持有、agent 只读**的文件。
    43	实测那个棕地项目升到 4.1 之后，`process-config.md` **仍是模板原样**（只改了 language / cas / caps），
    44	`id-pattern` 行**从未被加过**。修复躺在二进制里，落地率为 **0**。
    45	
    46	### 1.2 doctor D6 的修复提示指向了错误的对象
    47	
    48	`lib/doctor.js:147`：
    49	
    50	```js
    51	d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding',
    52	  detail: `scenario(s) without a bindable ... ID${src}: ...`, fix: 'add leading IDs' });
    53	```
    54	
    55	在上面那个项目里，那 50 个 ID **完全合法**，问题在正则。
    56	按提示做，人会去改 50 个 ID；正确动作是加一行 config（或者，本 change 之后，什么都不用做）。
    57	**错误的 fix 提示比没有提示更贵。**
    58	
    59	### 1.3 delta 的语法里没有「说明性内容」的位置（复盘 FP-2）
    60	
    61	`lib/archive-merge.js` 的 delta 解析器（`parseDeltaStrict`）只认三种 h2 段
    62	（`## ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED|RENAMED Requirements`）。作者写的说明被当成指令解析：
    63	
    64	| 现象 | 位置 | 性质 |
    65	|---|---|---|
    66	| `## 场景 ID 逐条映射` 这类说明性小节 → `unrecognized section heading`，归档被阻断 | `:127` | **响亮**的误判——作者被迫降级标题或改写成段内附注（绕过而非表达） |
    67	| **`### 非-Requirement 标题` 落在 requirement 块内 → 被 `blockLines.push(line)` 当作正文并入，最终写进 store** | `:166` | **静默**的错误——比响亮的那个更危险 |
    68	
    69	第二条是我在读源码时自查发现的，复盘没提。它的形状是：
    70	
    71	```js
    72	:166  if (state === 'IN_REQUIREMENT') { blockLines.push(line); continue; }   // scenarios and prose are body
    73	```
    74	
    75	`### 说明` 不匹配 `REQ_LINE_RE`（`/^###\s+Requirement:\s+(.+?)\s*$/`），也不是 h2，
    76	于是在 `IN_REQUIREMENT` 状态下被当成正文，**原样并进 store 的 Requirement 块**。
    77	MODIFIED 路径上 4.1.0 的 MODIFIED INTEGRITY 报告能照出来（它逐行比对被替换的块），
    78	**ADDED 路径上照不出来**——没有旧块可比。
    79	
    80	### 1.4 状态 A 的其余事实（决定范围与风险）
    81	
    82	**`DEFAULT_ID` 的全部消费者**（本次 grep 实证）：
    83	
    84	| 消费者 | 位置 | 用途 |
    85	|---|---|---|
    86	| `resolveIdPattern` 的兜底 | `lib/config.js:131` | flag > config 行 > **DEFAULT_ID** |
    87	| `check` 的 CK-04 | `lib/check.js:163` `checkScenarioIds(specText, name, idPattern = DEFAULT_ID)` | 结构一致性检查 |
    88	| `doctor` 的 D5 探针 | `lib/doctor.js:33` `const idRe = new RegExp(DEFAULT_ID)` | TAP 解析。`truth/doctor.md` 称其 count 分类「pattern-insensitive」——**该声称已被 r1 用反例推翻**，见 §1.5 |
    89	| `doctor` 的 D6 | 经 `resolveIdPattern`（config > 默认） | 库健康 |
    90	| `spec-runner` 的 verify / gate C1 | 经 `resolveIdPattern` | 绑定门 |
    91	
    92	**本仓自身的 store 对默认值变更免疫（本次实测）**：
    93	
    94	| pattern | identified | unidentified | duplicates |
    95	|---|---|---|---|
    96	| 现行 `[A-Z]+-\d+` | 353 | 0 | 0 |
    97	| 提议 `[A-Z]+(?:-[A-Z]+)*-\d+[a-z]*` | 353 | 0 | 0 |
    98	
    99	且两者产出的 **ID 集合逐个相同**。本仓的既有测试因此不会因这条改动而变色。
   100	
   101	**delta 语料对语法变更免疫（本次实测）**：
   102	`apriori/changes/archive/*/specs/**/*.md` 里
   103	——非 `### Requirement:` 的 h3 共 **0** 处；非三种法定 h2 的段共 **0** 处。
   104	`apriori/specs/` 的 store 里非 `### Requirement:` 的 h3 也是 **0** 处。
   105	**没有任何存量产物需要迁移。**
   106	
   107	### 1.5 D5 的「pattern-insensitive」是假的（r1·REQ-1，已复核）
   108	
   109	`lib/doctor.js:45` 的计数是：
   110	
   111	```js
   112	const parsed = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0)
   113	             + untagged.length + unattributedFailures.length;
   114	```
   115	
   116	**被打了 tag 的 SKIP / TODO 贡献 0**（`r.pass` 与 `r.fail` 都是 0，且它不在 `untagged` 里）。于是：
   117	
   118	| TAP 行 | 旧 pattern | 新 pattern |
   119	|---|---|---|
   120	| `ok 1 - AC-30f pending # SKIP flaky` | `AC-30f` 绑不上 → 进 `untagged` → `parsed = 1` → **D5 ok** | 绑上了 → 只剩 `skip: 1` → `parsed = 0` → **D5 finding「truncated or malformed」** |
   121	
   122	`AC-BIS-01` 与 `not ok … # TODO` 有同样的反例。
   123	**所以「只换一个常量」会让某些合法的 TAP plumbing 从 HEALTHY 变成 FINDINGS**——
   124	而 AC-IP-09 恰恰要求它不变。两者不可兼得，必须在需求阶段裁掉一个。
   125	
   126	> 注意：这个计数怪癖**今天就存在**——`ok 1 - AC-01 x # SKIP` 这类**旧 pattern 也认得**的
   127	> tagged skip，今天同样会让 `parsed = 0`。本 change 并没有造出它，只是会让更多行落进那一格。
   128	
   129	---
   130	
   131	## 二、目标状态 B
   132	
   133	### B1 — 默认 id-pattern 放宽到认得真实世界的 ID 形状
   134	
   135	新默认值：
   136	
   137	```
   138	[A-Z]+(?:-[A-Z]+)*-\d+[a-z]*
   139	```
   140	
   141	- 是现行默认式的**严格超集**（`AC-01` 仍匹配，且匹配到的**子串完全相同**）；
   142	- 认得三段及以上（`AC-BIS-01`、`LIFE-DWS-01`）；
   143	- 认得字母后缀（`AC-30f`）——与 `leadId` 的边界规则相容（整个 ID 被吃掉，其后是空格）。
   144	
   145	**不改 `leadId` 的边界规则**（见 §五 O3）。
   146	
   147	### B1b — D5 与项目的场景 ID 词汇表**解耦**（r1·REQ-1 的处置）
   148	
   149	`doctor` 的 D5 只是在判「这条命令说不说 TAP」这个**管道**问题，它不关心项目的 ID 长什么样。
   150	它今天恰好借用了 `DEFAULT_ID`，于是被本 change 牵连。
   151	
   152	**处置：D5 不再是 `DEFAULT_ID` 的消费者**——它改用一个**属于自己的、冻结的**分类正则，
   153	其值就是本 change 之前的 `[A-Z]+-\d+`，并在源码与 KB 里写明理由：
   154	「D5 判的是管道，不是身份；它的分类刻意独立于项目的场景 ID 词汇表」。
   155	
   156	这样 AC-IP-09 从「一个未经验证的声称」变成**结构上必然成立**的事实：
   157	D5 根本不读那个常量，放宽它当然影响不到 D5。
   158	
   159	**不做的另一支**：把 D5 的计数改成基于 lexer 的 point/skip/todo 结构量。
   160	那会**改变 D5 今天的行为**（上面那条注记里的既有怪癖会被一并修掉），
   161	是一件独立的、有自己验收面的事——记入 O8。
   162	
   163	### B2 — doctor D6 按「ID 长什么样」给不同的修复提示
   164	
   165	D6 报 unidentified 时，必须先分辨这两类，给出**不同**的 fix：
   166	
   167	**分类契约（r1·REQ-3 的处置——「形似」必须有可执行定义）**：
   168	
   169	1. 取场景标题，`trim()` 之后按**第一个空白**切出**首 token**；
   170	2. 首 token 是「**ID 形状**」当且仅当：它**含至少一个数字** **且** 含至少一个 `-` 或 `_`。
   171	   （`AC-BIS-01` ✅ · `ac-01` ✅ · `AC_01` ✅ · `AC-30f` ✅ · `AC-01:` ✅（冒号不影响这两个条件）
   172	   · `123` ❌ 无分隔符 · `AC-` ❌ 无数字 · 空标题 ❌）
   173	3. 生效 matcher 对该标题返回 null 时：
   174	   - 首 token 是 ID 形状 → **pattern-mismatch** 类；
   175	   - 否则 → **missing-ID** 类。
   176	
   177	| 类 | fix 指向 |
   178	|---|---|
   179	| **missing-ID** | 「给场景加上前导 ID」——现状文案，**一字不改** |
   180	| **pattern-mismatch** | 「这些 ID 看起来是合法的，只是不匹配当前生效的 pattern；改 `apriori/process-config.md` 的 `id-pattern` 行」 |
   181	
   182	**detail 的输出契约**：pattern-mismatch 类的 detail 必须同时给出
   183	① 生效 pattern 的 **bounded source**（沿用既有的 `boundedSource`）与 ② 其 **origin**（`flag`/`config`/`default`）；
   184	样例最多 **3** 个，按**库内出现顺序**取前三，每个截断到 40 字符（沿用既有 D6 的截断惯例）。
   185	
   186	**finding 条数契约**：两类各产出**恰好一条** D6 finding（各自点名自己的场景），
   187	互不合并；`findings` 计数按既有规则 = finding 状态条目数，因此两类同时存在时比今天多一条。
   188	
   189	### B3 — delta 给说明性内容一个合法位置，并堵掉静默并入
   190	
   191	1. **一个被解析器忽略的 h2 段**：`## Notes`。它的整段内容不参与任何合并，也不产生 problem。
   192	
   193	   **`IN_NOTES` 的完整转移契约（r1·REQ-4 的处置）**：
   194	
   195	   | 问题 | 裁定 |
   196	   |---|---|
   197	   | 可以出现在哪里 | 第一个操作段**之前**、两个操作段**之间**、文件**末尾** —— 三处皆可 |
   198	   | 可以出现几次 | **不限次** |
   199	   | 进入时 | `flush()` 当前 requirement 块（与其它 h2 一致），状态转 `IN_NOTES` |
   200	   | 何时结束 | **围栏之外**的下一个 h2。若它是**四种**法定操作段之一（`ADDED` / `MODIFIED` / `REMOVED` / **`RENAMED`**——r2 勘误：v2 写成「三种」）→ 正常恢复解析；若是其它 h2 → 按 AC-IP-16 照旧报 problem |
   201	   | 段内的 `### Requirement:` / `#### Scenario:` | **全部忽略**，不产生任何合并操作、不产生 problem |
   202	   | 段内的 `<!-- apriori-base: … -->` | **全部忽略**：既不被采纳为 CAS 戳，也不因「重复戳」「戳在段后」而报 problem |
   203	   | Notes 标题本身是否置 `sectionSeen` | **否**——但这不代表戳可以跟在 Notes 后面，见下一行 |
   204	| 首个操作段**之前**的合法 CAS 戳该放哪 | **必须放在 `## Notes` 之前**。r2 指出 v2 自相矛盾：Notes 只在下一个 h2 处结束，所以「Notes 之后、首个操作段之前」的那个戳**仍在 Notes 内**，按「段内戳一律忽略」就该被忽略，而 AC-IP-14d 又要求它有效——两者不可兼得。裁定取**「Notes 真正不透明」**这一支：戳前置。（另一支是给 Notes 发明一个显式终止语法，那会扩大本 change 的语法面，不做） |
   205	   | 段内的代码围栏 | 照旧不透明；**未闭合的围栏**使其后内容保持不透明直到文件末尾（与既有 fence 语义一致） |
   206	   | Notes-only 的 delta（除 Notes 外零操作） | **仍按零操作拒绝** —— `deltaOpCount === 0` 的既有契约（`lib/archive-merge.js:375` / `:815`，D-AM-3）**一字不动** |
   207	2. **requirement 块内的非-`Requirement` h3 变成一个 problem**（带行号），不再静默并入 store。
   208	
   209	   **标题层级 × 状态矩阵（r1·REQ-5 的处置——只定义一个状态是不够的）**：
   210	
   211	   **r2 勘误**：v2 的矩阵把 `RENAMED` 当成一个 state，而它其实是**正交的 `kind`**；
   212	   而且漏了真实存在的 `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` 状态。于是「RENAMED 段内非法 Requirement 之后」
   213	   这个真实复合态（`state=IN_REQUIREMENT` + `kind=RENAMED` + `blockDiscard=true`）
   214	   会同时命中「`IN_REQUIREMENT` → 新 problem」和「`RENAMED` → 自由文本不变」两格，给出相反结果。
   215	
   216	   改为按**真实的 `state`**（外加两条优先级规则）表述：
   217	
   218	   | 行 | `FILE_PREAMBLE` | `IN_SECTION`（任一 kind 的段首自由文本） | `IN_REQUIREMENT` | `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` | `IN_NOTES` | 围栏内 |
   219	   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   220	   | `### Requirement: …` | 照旧 problem（块在段前） | 照旧开块；`kind === 'RENAMED'` 时照旧 problem + 作废块 | 照旧开新块 | 照旧被原 h2 的 problem 覆盖，静默跳过 | 忽略 | 不透明 |
   221	   | **其它 `### …`** | **照旧自由文本** | **照旧自由文本** | **新 problem**（受下方优先级 P1 约束） | 照旧静默跳过 | 忽略 | 不透明 |
   222	   | `#### Scenario: …` | 照旧 problem | 照旧 problem | 照旧正文 | 照旧静默跳过 | 忽略 | 不透明 |
   223	   | `##### …` 及更深 | 照旧自由文本 / 正文 | 同左 | 同左 | 照旧静默跳过 | 忽略 | 不透明 |
   224	   | `## Notes` | 进 `IN_NOTES` | 进 `IN_NOTES`（先 flush） | 进 `IN_NOTES`（先 flush） | **进 `IN_NOTES`**（h2 结束跳过态） | 重开一个 Notes 段 | 不透明 |
   225	
   226	   **两条优先级规则（必须钉死，否则 buckets 与 problems 会因实现选择而不同）**：
   227	
   228	   - **P1 —— `blockDiscard === true` 只优先于「新 h3」这一项检查**（r3 勘误）：
   229	     当前 block 已被作废时，**本 change 新增的那条 h3 判定不再触发**，该行按状态 A 落进 body 被静默吸收，
   230	     **不产出第二条 problem**。
   231	     **但 P1 绝不覆盖状态 A 在 body 分支之前的其它行处理**——尤其是 **CAS 戳**：
   232	     `STAMP_ATTEMPT_LINE_RE` 在状态 A 里于**每一个非跳过状态**都先于 `REQ_LINE_RE` 与 body 分支被匹配
   233	     （「一个游离的戳绝不被当作正文吸收」是它已有的保证）。v3 的「后续**所有**非-h2、非合法-Requirement
   234	     行继续静默吸收」会把戳一并吞掉，那正好破坏「RENAMED 逐字节不变」这条承诺。
   235	     **正确表述**：P1 是对**新增判定**的抑制，不是对状态 A 行处理顺序的改写。
   236	     这一条同时覆盖两个作废块来源：① 本 change 新增的「非-Requirement h3 作废块」；
   237	     ② 既有的「RENAMED 段内非法 Requirement 作废块」——后者行为因此**一字不变**。
   238	   - **P2 —— 出错后的恢复**：记录**一条** problem、置 `blockDiscard = true`、停留在 `IN_REQUIREMENT`，
   239	     直到下一个 `### Requirement:` 或任一 h2 才恢复。被作废的 block **不进** buckets。
   240	
   241	   **只收紧一格**：`IN_REQUIREMENT` 且 `blockDiscard === false` 时的非-`Requirement` h3。其余一律保持状态 A。
   242	3. RUNBOOK 的 delta 模板给出 `## Notes` 的示例。
   243	
   244	---
   245	
   246	## 三、验收标准
   247	
   248	### 默认 pattern（A 组）
   249	
   250	| ID | 若 | 则 |
   251	|---|---|---|
   252	| AC-IP-01 | 一个场景标题为 `AC-BIS-01 …` / `LIFE-DWS-01 …` / `AC-30f …`，在**未配置** `id-pattern` 的项目里跑 `verify` | 三者都被识别为已绑定 ID（今日全部 unidentified） |
   253	| AC-IP-02 | 现行默认式能识别的**任何**输入 | 新默认式识别出**逐字节相同**的 ID（严格超集，且不改变已有绑定） |
   254	| AC-IP-03 | 本仓自身的 store，分别用新旧默认式跑 `collectScenarios` | identified / unidentified / duplicates 三个计数与 **ID 集合**全部相同 |
   255	| AC-IP-04 | 一个标题没有任何前导 ID 的场景 | 仍为 unidentified（放宽不等于放弃） |
   256	| AC-IP-05 | 项目**配置了** `id-pattern` 行 | 配置仍然优先，默认值不参与——`resolveIdPattern` 的优先级（flag > config > 默认）一字不变 |
   257	| AC-IP-06 | 一个项目里同时存在 `AC-30` 与 `AC-30f` | 两者是**两个不同的 ID**，不构成重复 |
   258	| AC-IP-07 | 放宽后新出现的重复 ID（两个此前都 unidentified、现在都识别成同一 ID 的场景） | 照常报 duplicate——这是**期望的新失败**，须在 CHANGELOG 声明 |
   259	| AC-IP-08 | `check` 的 CK-04、`doctor` 的 D6、`verify`、`gate` C1 四个消费者 | 全部经由**同一个** `DEFAULT_ID`，无第二处硬编码 |
   260	| AC-IP-09 | `doctor` 的 D5 探针（`lib/doctor.js:33`） | 其 TAP 计数分类的结论**不因默认值放宽而改变**（`truth/doctor.md` 声称它 pattern-insensitive——本条把该声称变成断言） |
   261	
   262	### doctor D6 的 fix 提示（B 组）
   263	
   264	| ID | 若 | 则 |
   265	|---|---|---|
   266	| AC-IP-10 | store 里有场景标题带形似 ID 的前导 token，但不匹配当前生效 pattern | D6 的 fix **指向 `process-config.md` 的 `id-pattern` 行**，detail 回显样例 ID 与当前 pattern 的来源 |
   267	| AC-IP-11 | store 里有场景标题**完全没有**前导 ID token | D6 的 fix 仍是「加前导 ID」，文案与本 change 前一致 |
   268	| AC-IP-12 | 两类同时存在 | 两条 fix 都出现，各自点名自己的场景，不混为一谈 |
   269	| AC-IP-13 | D6 的其余行为（重复 ID、空库、缺目录、坏 pattern 配置） | **一字不变** |
   270	
   271	### delta 语法（C 组）
   272	
   273	| ID | 若 | 则 |
   274	|---|---|---|
   275	| AC-IP-14 | 一个**其余部分合法且至少含一个操作**的 delta 含一个 `## Notes` 段 | `archive` 与 `verify --change` 正常通过；该段内容**不参与合并**、**不写进 store**、**不产生 problem** |
   276	| AC-IP-14b | 一个**只有** `## Notes`、没有任何操作的 delta | **仍按零操作拒绝**（既有 `parsed 0 delta operations` 文案不变）——Notes 不是操作 |
   277	| AC-IP-14c | `## Notes` 分别出现在首个操作段**之前** / 两个操作段**之间** / 文件**末尾**；以及出现**两次** | 四种都正常通过，段内内容一律不参与合并 |
   278	| AC-IP-14d | 合法 CAS 戳出现在 `## Notes` **之前** | 戳正常生效（Notes 标题不置 `sectionSeen`，因此不触发「戳在段后」的 problem） |
   279	| AC-IP-14d2 | 合法形状的 CAS 戳出现在 `## Notes` **段内** | 被**忽略**：既不采纳为戳，也不报 problem。若该 delta 有 mutation 操作且别处没有戳，它就是**未打戳**，按既有 CAS 默认拒绝路径处理 |
   280	| AC-IP-14g | `## Notes` 之后紧跟 `## RENAMED Requirements` | 正常恢复解析（四种法定操作段都恢复，不只三种） |
   281	| AC-IP-14e | `## Notes` 段结束于**围栏之外**的下一个 h2 | 若是法定操作段 → 正常恢复解析；若是其它 h2 → 照旧报 problem（AC-IP-16） |
   282	| AC-IP-14f | `## Notes` 段内含**未闭合**的代码围栏 | 其后内容保持不透明直到文件末尾，与既有 fence 语义一致 |
   283	| AC-IP-15 | `## Notes` 段内出现 `### Requirement:` / `#### Scenario:` / `<!-- apriori-base: … -->` | 全部被忽略（Notes 段是不透明的），不产生额外的合并操作、不打乱 CAS 戳的判定 |
   284	| AC-IP-16 | delta 含**其它**未识别的 h2（如 `## 场景 ID 逐条映射`） | 仍是带行号的 problem，文案与本 change 前一致——只有 `## Notes` 是新开的口子 |
   285	| AC-IP-17 | requirement 块内出现非-`Requirement` 的 h3（如 `### 说明`） | **成为带行号的 problem**，该 delta 被拒绝；今天它会被静默并入 store |
   286	| AC-IP-17b | 同上，且该 h3 之后还有若干行正文，再跟一个合法的 `### Requirement:` | 该 h3 只产出**一条** problem（不刷屏）；被作废的块**不进** buckets；其后的合法 Requirement 正常开新块 |
   287	| AC-IP-17c | 非-`Requirement` h3 出现在 `FILE_PREAMBLE` / `IN_SECTION` 段首自由文本处 | **行为与本 change 前一字不变**（自由文本） |
   288	| AC-IP-17e | `RENAMED` 段内先出现非法 `### Requirement:`（触发既有的作废块），其后再出现非-`Requirement` h3 | 由**优先级 P1** 裁定：静默吸收，**不产出第二条 problem**——RENAMED 段的既有行为一字不变（r2 勘误：v2 的矩阵在此给出相反结果） |
   289	| AC-IP-17f | 非-`Requirement` h3 出现在 `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` 状态（前面有个未识别 h2） | 照旧被那个 h2 的 problem 覆盖，静默跳过，不新增 problem |
   290	| AC-IP-17g | `## Notes` 出现在 `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` 状态 | 结束跳过态并进入 `IN_NOTES`（h2 是跳过态的终止条件） |
   291	| AC-IP-17h | **作废块内**出现一个 CAS 戳形状的行（两种作废来源各一：新增的 h3 作废块、既有的 RENAMED 非法-Requirement 作废块） | 该行仍按**状态 A** 的戳处理走（被采纳、或按重复/位置报 problem），**不被当作正文吸收**——P1 只抑制新增的 h3 判定，不改状态 A 的行处理顺序（r3 勘误） |
   292	| AC-IP-17d | `#####` 及更深的标题，出现在任一状态 | 行为与本 change 前一字不变 |
   293	| AC-IP-18 | 同上，但在 `ADDED` 段（无旧块可比） | 同样被拒绝——这正是 MODIFIED INTEGRITY 报告**照不到**的那一路 |
   294	| AC-IP-19 | `apriori/changes/archive/**` 的全部存量 delta 语料 | 逐个仍**逐字节等价**地解析（既有语料测试不得变色；实测语料中两种构造各 0 处） |
   295	| AC-IP-20 | 代码围栏内的 `### …` 与 `## …` | 仍然不透明，不受本 change 影响 |
   296	
   297	### 文档（D 组）
   298	
   299	| ID | 若 | 则 |
   300	|---|---|---|
   301	| AC-IP-21 | 读 `templates/process-config.md` | `id-pattern` 行的 **Value 列**（第 2 列，会被 `init` 原样写进新项目并由 `resolveIdPattern` **优先消费**）**与 Default 列（第 4 列）都**与新默认式一致；表下注释里「built-in default `[A-Z]+-\d+`」的字样一并更新（r1·REQ-2：只改 Default 列的话，新项目会带着**旧** pattern 的 active config 行，把新 `DEFAULT_ID` 完全遮蔽，而静态文档验收却仍然通过） |
   302	| AC-IP-21b | 全新 `apriori init` 之后**不手改** config，跑 `resolveIdPattern` | 返回**新** source，`origin: 'config'`；且 config 来源的匹配子进程对 `AC-BIS-01` / `LIFE-DWS-01` / `AC-30f` 的**场景标题与 TAP description** 都返回完整 ID |
   303	| AC-IP-21c | 同一个全新项目上跑 `check` / `doctor` D6 / `verify` / `gate` C1 | 四者对上述三种 ID 的结论一致（同一条生效 pattern，无第二处硬编码） |
   304	| AC-IP-22 | 读 `RUNBOOK.md` / `RUNBOOK_cn.md` 的 delta 相关段落 | 出现 `## Notes` 的用法说明与一个示例 |
   305	| AC-IP-23 | 读 `docs/cli.md` / `docs/cli_cn.md` 中提及默认 id-pattern 的位置 | 与新默认式一致 |
   306	| AC-IP-24 | 读 `CHANGELOG.md` | 显著声明**默认值变更**（可能让既有项目新出现 duplicate 报告）与 **delta 语法的两处变更** |

   309	
   310	## 四、明确不做（out of scope）
   311	
   312	| # | 不做 | 理由 |
   313	|---|---|---|
   314	| O1 | 改 `resolveIdPattern` 的优先级（flag > config > 默认） | 那是 `gate-id-pattern` 刚立的契约，本 change 只换兜底值 |
   315	| O2 | 把 `id-pattern` 行写进用户的 `process-config.md` | 违反 R3；而且本 change 的**要点**正是让大多数项目**不需要**那一行 |
   316	| O3 | 改 `leadId` 的边界规则 | 它是「ID 后面不能紧跟字母数字下划线」这条独立保证，与 pattern 宽窄正交；改它会影响每一个消费者的绑定语义 |
   317	| O4 | 让解析器「智能判断」哪些 h2/h3 是说明、哪些是指令 | 复盘明确列为过度方案。**一个**约定好的段名，就够了 |
   318	| O5 | 给 `## Notes` 之外再开别的忽略段 | YAGNI；需要时是另一个 change |
   319	| O6 | 回溯修补存量 store 里被静默并入的说明性内容 | 实测为 **0** 处，无事可修 |
   320	| O7 | change 2（`archive-preflight`）的任何内容 | 它已挂起；本 change 只碰 delta **解析器**，与它改的高层 archive 块不是同一个 Requirement |
   321	| O8 | 修 D5 计数把 tagged SKIP/TODO 算成 0 这个**既有**怪癖（§1.5 的注记） | 它今天就存在、与本 change 无因果关系；修它要改 D5 的分类语义，有自己的验收面。本 change 用 B1b 的**解耦**保证自己不把它变严重，仅此而已 |
   322	| O9 | 讨论「模板到底该不该给 `id-pattern` 设一个 active Value」 | r1·REQ-2 暴露出：模板设了 Value，就意味着每个新项目都走 config 来源的子进程路径。这是个**独立的**产品决定（要动 `init` 的模板哲学），本 change 只保证两列一致、不留旧值 |
   323	
   324	**触及范围（源码）**：`lib/config.js`（`DEFAULT_ID` 一个常量）、`lib/doctor.js`（**D5 的分类 regex 解耦** + D6 的 fix 分支——r2 勘误：v2 此处漏了已成为目标状态的 D5 解耦）、
   325	`lib/archive-merge.js`（`## Notes` 忽略段 + 非-Requirement h3 的 problem）。
   326	
   327	---
   328	
   329	## 五、已知后果与风险
   330	
   331	| # | 事项 | 处置 |
   332	|---|---|---|
   333	| K1 | **默认值变更是外部可见的行为变更**：既有项目里此前 unidentified 的场景会变成已识别；若其中两个恰好识别成同一 ID，会**新出现** duplicate 报错 | 期望行为（重复 ID 本来就是缺陷，只是此前被 unidentified 掩盖）。必须在 CHANGELOG 显著声明（AC-IP-24） |
   334	| K2 | 此前 unidentified 的场景变成 **UNBOUND**（识别了但没有对应测试） | 同样是期望行为：从「这条我看不懂」变成「这条你没测」——后者才是真话 |
   335	| K3 | `doctor` D5 **不再**消费 `DEFAULT_ID`（B1b 已裁定解耦），改用属于自己的冻结分类 regex | AC-IP-09 因此从「一个待验证的声称」变成**结构上必然**成立：D5 根本不读那个常量。验收方式是「改变 `DEFAULT_ID` 前后 D5 的结论一致」。**r2 勘误**：v2 此处仍留着 v1 的「D5 用 DEFAULT_ID / 若不成立则 STEP2 重新设计」，与 B1b 直接冲突 |
   336	| K4 | `## Notes` 是新的**语法面**，一旦发布就要长期兼容 | 段名与语义在本 change 里定死；O5 声明不再开第二个 |
   337	| K5 | 非-Requirement h3 由「静默并入」变「拒绝」是**收紧** | 实测存量语料 0 处受影响；但外部项目可能有——CHANGELOG 需声明，并给出「改成 `## Notes` 或降级为正文」的迁移建议 |
   338	| K6 | 与挂起的 change 2 的 CAS 戳冲突 | 本 change 归档后，`archive-preflight` 的 archive-merge delta 需换戳。已记在其 flow-state |
   339

   342	## 七、STEP0·r3 处置说明（本版）
   343	
   344	| ID | 处置 | 说明 |
   345	|---|---|---|
   346	| REQ-5（第三次 reopened） | **accept** | 评审方确认真实 state / RENAMED kind / `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` / `IN_NOTES` 的建模与普通 h3 的 discard/recovery 都对了；剩下的是 **P1 的措辞太宽**：我写「后续**所有**非-h2、非合法-Requirement 行继续静默吸收」，而状态 A 在**每一个非跳过状态**都会**先**匹配 `STAMP_ATTEMPT_LINE_RE` 再进 body 分支（「游离的戳绝不被当正文吸收」是它既有的保证）。按 v3 的字面实现会把作废块内的戳一并吞掉——恰好打破我自己承诺的「RENAMED 逐字节不变」。改为：**P1 是对新增 h3 判定的抑制，不是对状态 A 行处理顺序的改写**；新增 AC-IP-17h 对两种作废来源各构造一个戳形状行来钉死 |
   347	| REQ-1 / 2 / 3 / 4 | r3 已全部 **verified** | — |

   351	## 七·2、STEP0·r2 处置说明
   352	
   353	| ID | 处置 | 说明 |
   354	|---|---|---|
   355	| REQ-1（reopened） | **accept** | 评审方确认 B1b 的方向成立、且移除 D5 后没有第二个暴露点；重开的原因是我在 v2 里**留下了两处 v1 的旧话**——K3 仍写「D5 用 DEFAULT_ID / 若不成立则 STEP2 重新设计」，触及范围仍只写「D6 的 fix 分支」。同一份需求于是同时要求「现在解耦」和「以后再决定」。两处都已改正 |
   356	| REQ-4（reopened） | **accept**，取「Notes 真正不透明」一支 | 真矛盾：Notes 只在下一个 h2 结束，所以「Notes 之后、首个操作段之前」的戳**仍在 Notes 内**，与「段内戳一律忽略」直接冲突。裁定：**首个操作段前的合法戳必须放在 `## Notes` 之前**；AC-IP-14d 改写，另加 AC-IP-14d2 明确「段内戳被忽略 ⇒ 若别处无戳则该 delta 视为未打戳，走既有 CAS 默认拒绝」。另一支（给 Notes 发明显式终止语法）会扩大语法面，不做。同时把「三种法定操作段」改正为**四种**（漏了 `RENAMED`），新增 AC-IP-14g |
   357	| REQ-5（reopened） | **accept** | 两处硬伤：① `RENAMED` 不是 state 而是**正交的 `kind`**，所以「RENAMED 段内非法 Requirement 之后」这个真实复合态会同时命中矩阵两格并给出相反结果；② 漏了真实存在的 `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` 状态。矩阵改为按**真实 state** 表述并补上 `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` 与 `## Notes` 行，另加两条优先级规则：**P1** `blockDiscard === true` 优先于新 h3 检查（同时覆盖新旧两种作废块来源，RENAMED 的既有行为因此一字不变）、**P2** 出错恢复。新增 AC-IP-17e/17f/17g |
   358	| REQ-2 / REQ-3 | r2 已 **verified**，本版未改动其对应内容 | — |

   362	## 七·3、STEP0·r1 处置说明
   363	
   364	| ID | 处置 | 说明 |
   365	|---|---|---|
   366	| REQ-1（high） | **accept**，取「解耦」一支 | 反例成立且已复核：`parsed` 的算式（`lib/doctor.js:45`）里**被打 tag 的 SKIP/TODO 贡献 0**，所以放宽默认式会让 `ok 1 - AC-30f x # SKIP` 这类行从 `untagged`（parsed=1，D5 ok）变成 tagged skip（parsed=0，D5 finding）。§1.5 把反例写进状态 A。处置**不是**「STEP2 再说」，而是在需求阶段裁定：**D5 不再消费 `DEFAULT_ID`**，改用属于自己的冻结分类正则（值即本 change 前的 `[A-Z]+-\d+`），理由写进源码与 KB——D5 判的是**管道**不是**身份**。这样 AC-IP-09 从「一个声称」变成**结构上必然**成立。另一支（把计数改成基于 lexer 的结构量）会顺带改掉 D5 今天的行为，记入 **O8** 不做 |
   367	| REQ-2（high） | **accept** | 真缺口：`templates/process-config.md` 的 `id-pattern` 行**第 2 列（Value）**会被 `init` 原样写进新项目并由 `resolveIdPattern` **优先消费**。只改第 4 列（Default）的话，新项目带着**旧** pattern 的 active config，把新 `DEFAULT_ID` 完全遮蔽，而静态文档验收还是过的。AC-IP-21 改为**两列 + 表下注释**全改，并新增 AC-IP-21b（fresh init 后 `origin: config` 且 config 子进程认得三种 ID 形状）与 AC-IP-21c（四个消费者结论一致）。「模板该不该设 active Value」这个更上位的问题记入 **O9** |
   368	| REQ-3（med） | **accept** | 「形似 ID」给出可执行定义：`trim()` 后取首个空白分隔 token，**含至少一个数字 且 含至少一个 `-` 或 `_`** 即为 ID 形状（`ac-01`/`AC_01`/`AC-01:` ✅，`123`/`AC-` ❌）。另钉死 detail 必须同时输出 bounded source 与 origin、样例最多 3 个按库内顺序取前三各截 40 字符、两类各产出**恰好一条** finding |
   369	| REQ-4（high） | **accept** | 给出完整 `IN_NOTES` 转移契约：可出现在三处且不限次；进入时 flush；结束于**围栏外**的下一个 h2（法定段恢复解析、其它 h2 照旧报 problem）；段内的 Requirement/Scenario/CAS 戳**全部忽略且不产生 problem**；Notes 标题**不置** `sectionSeen`（所以戳可以在 Notes 之后）；未闭合围栏保持不透明。**AC-IP-14 改写**为「其余部分合法且至少含一个操作」，并新增 AC-IP-14b 明确 **Notes-only 仍按零操作拒绝**——这正是 r1 指出的与 D-AM-3 的冲突 |
   370	| REQ-5（med） | **accept** | 给出**标题层级 × 状态**的完整矩阵：只有 `IN_REQUIREMENT` 里的非-`Requirement` h3 是新 problem，`FILE_PREAMBLE` / section preamble / `RENAMED` / h4+ **一律保持状态 A**。并钉死出错后的恢复策略：记一条 problem、**丢弃当前 block**（沿用 RENAMED 段的既有做法）、停留在 `IN_REQUIREMENT` 直到下一个 `### Requirement:` 或 h2 —— 既不写进 store，也不刷屏。新增 AC-IP-17b/17c/17d |
   371

    17	| `:156` | 导出 `DEFAULT_ID` 与 `resolveIdPattern` |

    19	### A2 `DEFAULT_ID` 的全部消费者（grep 实证，共 4 个真实消费点）
    20	
    21	| 消费者 | 位置 | 用途 | 本 change |
    22	|---|---|---|---|
    23	| `resolveIdPattern` 兜底 | `lib/config.js:131` | verify / gate C1 / doctor D6 的生效 pattern | **放宽**（B1） |
    24	| `check` CK-04 | `lib/check.js:163` `checkScenarioIds(specText, name, idPattern = DEFAULT_ID)` | 结构一致性 | 随之放宽 |
    25	| `doctor` D5 探针 | `lib/doctor.js:33` `const idRe = new RegExp(DEFAULT_ID)` | **TAP 行解析** | **解耦**（B1b） |
    26	| `spec-runner` 再导出 | `lib/spec-runner.js:18, :829` | 供 doctor 等取用 | 不变 |
    27	
    28	### A3 D5 的计数为什么会被牵连（req-final §1.5 的源码定位）
    29	
    30	```
    31	lib/doctor.js:45   const parsed = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0)
    32	                                + untagged.length + unattributedFailures.length;
    33	lib/doctor.js:50   if (parsed === 0) { … 'TAP stream truncated or malformed' … }
    34	```
    35	
    36	`parseTap` 把带 `# SKIP`/`# TODO` 的行记成 `skip`，**既不进 pass/fail、也不进 untagged**。
    37	于是「一个被识别了 ID 的 tagged skip」贡献 0。放宽默认式 ⇒ 更多行被识别 ⇒ 更多行落进这一格。
    38	
    39	**解耦点就是 `:33` 这一行**：把它换成本模块自己的冻结常量即可，`:45` 的算式一字不动。
    40	
    41	### A4 `lib/doctor.js` — D6 的 finding 构造
    42	
    43	| 位置 | 事实 |
    44	|---|---|
    45	| `:147` | `d6Entries.push({ id:'D6', status:'finding', detail: \`scenario(s) without a bindable ${boundedSource(idp.source)} ID${src}: …\`, fix: 'add leading IDs' })` |
    46	| — | detail **已经**回显 bounded source 与来源标记 `src`；本 change 要加的是**按类分流**与第二条 fix |
    47	| `:148` | 重复 ID 的 finding 与本 change 无关，须保持不变（AC-IP-13） |

    50	
    51	| 位置 | 事实 |
    52	|---|---|
    53	| `:81` | `SECTION_LINE_RE = /^##\s+(ADDED\|MODIFIED\|REMOVED\|RENAMED)\s+Requirements\s*$/` —— **四种**，不是三种 |
    54	| `:82` | `H2_LINE_RE = /^##\s/` |
    55	| `:83` | `REQ_LINE_RE = /^###\s+Requirement:\s+(.+?)\s*$/` |
    56	| `:100` | `let state = 'FILE_PREAMBLE';  // \| 'IN_SECTION' \| 'IN_REQUIREMENT' \| 'SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED'` —— **四个 state**；`kind` 是**正交**变量 |
    57	| `:118-125` | 围栏优先：`inFence` 时一切不透明 |
    58	| `:126` | `SECTION_LINE_RE` 命中 → flush、`state='IN_SECTION'`、`kind=…`、`sectionSeen=true` |
    59	| `:127-132` | 其它 h2 → problem + `state='SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED'`、`kind=null` |
    60	| `:133` | `if (state === 'SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED') continue;` —— **戳也被跳过**（注释明写 "stamps included"） |
    61	| `:135-144` | **`STAMP_ATTEMPT_LINE_RE` 在此**——先于 `REQ_LINE_RE`、先于 body 分支，在**每一个非跳过状态**都生效。这正是 r3 指出的、P1 不得覆盖的那一段 |
    62	| `:145-165` | `REQ_LINE_RE`：段前 → problem；`kind==='RENAMED'` → problem + **作废块**（`blockDiscard=true`）；否则开块（重名 → problem + 作废） |
    63	| `:166` | **`if (state === 'IN_REQUIREMENT') { blockLines.push(line); continue; }`** —— 静默并入的那一行；本 change 要在它**之前**插入新的 h3 判定 |
    64	| `:167` | `SCENARIO_LINE_RE` 在块外 → problem |
    65	| `:168-172` | `IN_SECTION` + `RENAMED` → 解析 `- Old -> New`；其余自由文本 |
    66	| `:375` / `:815` | `deltaOpCount(delta) === 0` → 零操作拒绝（Notes-only 必须仍走这条） |
    67	
    68	**插入点因此非常局部**：`:166` 之前加一条「`state==='IN_REQUIREMENT'` 且 `blockDiscard===false`
    69	且行匹配 `/^###\s/` 且不匹配 `REQ_LINE_RE`」的判定；加一个 `IN_NOTES` 状态与其转移。

     2	
     3	## MODIFIED Requirements
     4	
     5	### Requirement: the delta parser consumes its whole input
     6	`parseDeltaStrict` SHALL be a sequential, fully-consuming parser: every line (outside code fences, which are opaque) belongs to exactly one legal construct — file preamble (stamp/blank/free text without structure markers), a legal `## ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED|RENAMED Requirements` heading, section-preamble free text, a `### Requirement:` block (body runs to the next heading or EOF), a RENAMED `- Old -> New` line, or a `## Notes` section (opaque commentary: everything under it up to the next fence-outside h2 is ignored entirely — never merged, never a problem) — and anything else is a `problems[]` entry carrying its 1-based line number. The exhaustive problem set: unrecognized h2 headings (one problem per heading; its lines are never absorbed into any bucket), requirement/scenario markers before any section, a requirement block inside RENAMED, a scenario marker outside any requirement, duplicate requirement names, A NON-`Requirement` h3 INSIDE A REQUIREMENT BLOCK, and the stamp problems (which also gain line numbers). Both `verify --change` (exit 2) and `archive` (exit 1) inherit the failure.
     7	
     8	#### Scenario: AM-28 a misspelled section heading is reported, never absorbed
     9	- WHEN a delta contains `## ADDDED Requirements` (or any other unrecognized h2) followed by requirement blocks
    10	- THEN parsing reports the heading text with its line number, those requirements appear in NO bucket, and archive/verify fail closed
    11	
    12	#### Scenario: AM-29 structure outside its legal home is reported with line numbers
    13	- WHEN a `### Requirement:` appears before any section or inside a RENAMED section, or a `#### Scenario:` appears in a section preamble outside any requirement
    14	- THEN each is a problem carrying its 1-based line number; multiple distinct problems are all reported
    15	
    16	#### Scenario: AM-30 free text and fences stay legal; the corpus stays clean
    17	- WHEN a delta carries titles/notes in the file preamble or section preambles, prose (`#####` and deeper headings included) in requirement bodies, or fenced content containing heading-like lines
    18	- THEN no problem is reported — and every archived delta in this repo's `apriori/changes/archive/*/specs/` parses with zero problems (the regression corpus). An `###` heading that is not `### Requirement:` inside a requirement body is the ONE construct this list no longer covers: it is a problem, see the scenario below
    19	
    20	#### Scenario: AM-31 stamp problems carry line numbers
    21	- WHEN a delta carries a malformed, duplicated, or misplaced apriori-base stamp
    22	- THEN each stamp problem names its 1-based line number
    23	
    24	#### Scenario: AM-71 a non-Requirement h3 inside a block is a problem, not silent body text
    25	- WHEN a `###` heading that is not `### Requirement:` appears inside a requirement block — the shape an author reaches for when explaining WHY a block changed
    26	- THEN it is a problem carrying its line number and the delta is refused; today it is absorbed as body text and written verbatim into the living store, which the MODIFIED-integrity report can surface on a MODIFIED operation but never on an ADDED one, so the ADDED path corrupts the store silently
    27	- AND the same heading in the file preamble, in a section preamble, or in the skipped region after an unrecognized h2 keeps its existing behaviour; `####` and deeper headings inside a block stay body text
    28	
    29	#### Scenario: AM-72 one problem per bad block, and the stamp rule is untouched
    30	- WHEN a non-Requirement h3 is followed by more prose and then a legal `### Requirement:`
    31	- THEN exactly ONE problem is reported for that heading, the discarded block reaches no bucket, and the following legal requirement opens normally
    32	- AND WHEN a stamp-shaped line appears inside a discarded block — whether discarded by this rule or by the pre-existing illegal-requirement-inside-RENAMED rule — THEN it is still handled by the stamp rules, never absorbed as body text: discarding suppresses only the new h3 check, never the parser's line-handling order
    33	
    34	#### Scenario: AM-73 `## Notes` is opaque, and being opaque has consequences
    35	- WHEN a delta carries one or more `## Notes` sections — before the first operation section, between sections, or at end of file
    36	- THEN their content is ignored entirely: requirement and scenario markers inside them create no operations, a stamp-shaped line inside them is neither adopted nor reported, and no problem is raised; the section ends at the next fence-outside h2, where any of the four legal operation headings resumes parsing and any other h2 reports its own problem as before
    37	- AND a legal stamp intended for the delta must sit BEFORE `## Notes`, since a stamp after it is inside it and therefore ignored — leaving the delta unstamped and subject to the existing CAS default-deny
    38	- AND a delta consisting only of `## Notes` still fails the existing zero-operation guard: commentary is not an operation
apriori/changes/id-pattern-and-delta-notes/specs/doctor/spec.md
     1	<!-- apriori-base: sha256:2038c20e0d9dbe2fee74000a7edf3427512fa99b47c1496ec7f2c14bd1613504 -->
     2	
     3	## MODIFIED Requirements
     4	
     5	### Requirement: D6 shows its pattern source and diagnoses a bad config row
     6	`doctor`'s D6 SHALL scan the store with the effective id-pattern (config `id-pattern` row > `DEFAULT_ID`; doctor gains no flag) and its detail SHALL name the source — `config` when the row supplied the pattern, `default` otherwise. The id-pattern is resolved AND its D6 store scan is executed BEFORE the D5 probe runs (execution order; the report keeps its display order): an uncompilable config row — or a config-origin scan whose terminable child fails (timeout/kill/crash/malformed output) — makes D6 a `finding` that names `process-config` (fix: repair the row) WITHOUT throwing, AND D5 becomes `n/a` with the deterministic detail `probe skipped (invalid id-pattern config)` — the test command is never spawned under a broken id-pattern (the validate-before-any-test-command rule covers doctor too). Absent any other unusable condition the overall result stays `FINDINGS`, exit 1 — doctor diagnoses a repairable config, it does not refuse to run. D5's probe parsing SHALL NOT consume `DEFAULT_ID` at all: it keeps its own frozen classification regex, because it judges TAP PLUMBING rather than scenario identity, and borrowing the project's ID vocabulary made a widening of that vocabulary able to reclassify a healthy TAP stream — a tagged SKIP/TODO contributes nothing to the parsed count, so newly-recognised skips could turn `ok` into `truncated or malformed`. The earlier claim that the parsed-count classification is pattern-insensitive was FALSE; decoupling makes it true by construction. D6 SHALL also distinguish WHY a scenario is unbindable: a title whose first whitespace-delimited token contains at least one digit AND at least one `-` or `_` is ID-SHAPED, so its fix names the `id-pattern` row in `apriori/process-config.md`; a title without such a token gets the unchanged add-an-ID fix. Each class produces exactly one finding, and the pattern-mismatch detail carries both the bounded pattern source and its origin.
     7	
     8	#### Scenario: DR-16 D6 names its pattern source
     9	- WHEN doctor runs with a valid config `id-pattern` row covering the store's IDs, and separately without any row
    10	- THEN the first D6 is `ok` with a detail naming the config source, and the second names the default source (finding or ok per the store's IDs) — both source texts are asserted
    11	
    12	#### Scenario: DR-17 an invalid config id-pattern is a D6 finding and skips the probe
    13	- WHEN the config `id-pattern` row does not compile and doctor runs on an otherwise healthy project whose test command is a sentinel (it would leave a marker file if spawned)
    14	- THEN D6 is a `finding` naming `process-config` with a repair fix, no scenario scan happens, no exception escapes, D5 is `n/a` with detail `probe skipped (invalid id-pattern config)`, the sentinel marker does NOT exist (the command never ran), and the overall result is `FINDINGS` (exit 1)
    15	
    16	#### Scenario: DR-18 a terminated config-pattern scan is a D6 finding and skips the probe
    17	- WHEN the config pattern is catastrophic against the store's titles and doctor runs with a sentinel test command
    18	- THEN the D6 child scan is killed within its budget, D6 is a `finding` naming `process-config`, D5 is `n/a` (`probe skipped (invalid id-pattern config)`), the sentinel marker does NOT exist, and the overall result is `FINDINGS` (exit 1)
    19	
    20	#### Scenario: DR-19 D5 does not consume the project ID vocabulary
    21	- WHEN the effective default scenario-ID pattern is widened and a TAP stream carries a tagged skip whose description uses one of the newly-recognised ID shapes (for example `ok 1 - AC-30f pending # SKIP flaky`)
    22	- THEN D5's classification is IDENTICAL to what it was before the widening — it reads its own frozen regex, never `DEFAULT_ID`, so no widening of the project's ID vocabulary can move a healthy TAP stream into `truncated or malformed`
    23	
    24	#### Scenario: DR-20 D6 tells you which repair it means
    25	- WHEN the store holds scenarios whose titles carry an ID-shaped leading token that the effective pattern does not match, and separately scenarios with no such token at all
    26	- THEN two findings appear, each naming only its own scenarios: the first's fix points at the `id-pattern` row in `apriori/process-config.md` and its detail carries the bounded pattern source and its origin, the second's fix is the unchanged add-a-leading-ID text — a well-formed ID that merely misses the pattern must never send a human off to rewrite fifty IDs
apriori/changes/id-pattern-and-delta-notes/specs/spec-runner/spec.md
     1	<!-- apriori-base: sha256:307eec8f66291573e1d2361b5cf92ce8530e2f2dedbb4f12c4da9ddf79c7e8fb -->
     2	
     3	## MODIFIED Requirements
     4	
     5	### Requirement: spec-runner binds scenarios to test runs
     6	`apriori verify` SHALL enumerate scenario IDs from the spec store, run the project's own test command, parse its TAP output, and report each scenario's bound/green state. It SHALL exit 0 iff every identified scenario is bound to at least one test and all its tests pass, with no orphans or unidentified scenarios.
     7	
     8	#### Scenario: SR-01 every scenario bound and green
     9	- WHEN every spec scenario ID appears in a passing test name
    10	- THEN each is reported BOUND-GREEN and the process exits 0 with "spec is the test suite"
    11	
    12	#### Scenario: SR-02 a scenario with no test is UNBOUND
    13	- WHEN a spec scenario ID appears in no test name
    14	- THEN it is reported UNBOUND and the process exits 1
    15	
    16	#### Scenario: SR-03 a failing test makes its scenario BOUND-RED
    17	- WHEN a scenario's test emits `not ok`
    18	- THEN the scenario is reported BOUND-RED (with pass/fail counts) and the process exits 1
    19	
    20	#### Scenario: SR-04 one scenario, many tests — green iff all pass
    21	- WHEN a scenario ID leads multiple test names and at least one emits `not ok`
    22	- THEN the scenario is BOUND-RED; it is BOUND-GREEN only when all its tests pass
    23	
    24	#### Scenario: SR-05 a test with no matching scenario is ORPHAN
    25	- WHEN a test name leads with an ID that no spec scenario carries
    26	- THEN it is reported ORPHAN and the process exits 1
    27	
    28	#### Scenario: SR-06 a scenario with no ID is UNIDENTIFIED
    29	- WHEN a `#### Scenario:` heading has no leading ID matching the id-pattern
    30	- THEN it is reported UNIDENTIFIED (unbindable) and the process exits 1
    31	
    32	#### Scenario: SR-07 delegates execution to the project's own test command
    33	- WHEN `--test-cmd` is given any command that emits TAP
    34	- THEN the runner runs it as-is and never assumes a language or framework (TAP is the only coupling)
    35	
    36	#### Scenario: SR-08 the id-pattern is configurable; the default recognises multi-segment and suffixed IDs
    37	- WHEN `--id-pattern` is omitted
    38	- THEN IDs are matched as `[A-Z]+(?:-[A-Z]+)*-\d+[a-z]*` — a STRICT SUPERSET of the historical `[A-Z]+-\d+` that returns the byte-identical substring for every input the old one matched, and additionally recognises multi-segment IDs (`AC-BIS-01`, `LIFE-DWS-01`) and lowercase-suffixed ones (`AC-30f`), which real projects use and which the narrow default left permanently unbindable; when given, the configured pattern governs both spec and test extraction
    39	
    40	#### Scenario: SR-09 --json emits a machine-consumable verify report
    41	- WHEN `apriori verify … --json` runs
    42	- THEN it prints valid JSON — clean, result (GREEN/GAPS/ERROR), errors, exec (status/signal/error), duplicates, specFiles, boundGreen/boundRed (with pass/fail/skip counts), unbound, orphan, unidentified — and the exit code still encodes GREEN(0)/GAPS(1)/ERROR(2)
    43	
    44	#### Scenario: SR-10 zero parsed TAP results triggers a reporter hint
    45	- WHEN `apriori verify` runs a test command that produces output but not a single parseable TAP result line
    46	- THEN the report is preceded by a warning that the test command is probably not emitting TAP (naming node's `--test-reporter=tap`), so the all-UNBOUND list is not misread as missing tests
    47	
    48	#### Scenario: SR-11 execution status is part of the verdict
    49	- WHEN the test command fails to spawn, is killed by a signal, or exits non-zero while every parsed TAP result is green
    50	- THEN verify reports the run untrustworthy and exits 2 (never GREEN); a non-zero exit with matching red TAP remains ordinary GAPS (exit 1)
    51	
    52	#### Scenario: SR-12 vacuous inputs fail closed
    53	- WHEN a spec target does not exist, or the targets contain zero spec files, or the spec files contain zero scenarios
    54	- THEN verify reports the missing input and exits 2 — an empty universe is never a GREEN
    55	
    56	#### Scenario: SR-13 spec hygiene is enforced
    57	- WHEN the same scenario ID appears in more than one scenario heading, a scenario sits inside a markdown code fence, or a test/scenario name carries an ID followed by a word character — letter, digit or underscore (`XX-01b`, `XX-01_x`)
    58	- THEN duplicates are reported with their files and force GAPS; fenced scenarios are excluded as documentation; and the suffixed ID never binds to `XX-01` — it is unidentified/untagged instead of silently truncated
    59	
    60	#### Scenario: SR-14 TAP directives and aborts never count green
    61	- WHEN a TAP result carries a `# SKIP` or `# TODO` directive, or the output contains `Bail out!`
    62	- THEN skipped results count neither pass nor fail (a scenario with only skips stays UNBOUND), and a bailout makes the run an error (exit 2) regardless of earlier green lines
    63	
    64	#### Scenario: SR-15 --test-cmd falls back to the config
    65	- WHEN `apriori verify` is invoked without `--test-cmd` and `apriori/process-config.md` carries a `test-cmd` row (written by `apriori init --test-cmd`)
    66	- THEN that command is used; with neither present, usage exits 2
     1	# design — id-pattern-and-delta-notes
     2	
     3	> 对应 `requirement/req-final.md` 与 `gap-report.md` 的 G1..G8 / R1..R5。行号取自 f415824。
     4	
     5	---
     6	
     7	## D1 `lib/config.js` — 一个常量
     8	
     9	```
    10	:16   const DEFAULT_ID = '[A-Z]+-\\d+';
    11	   →  const DEFAULT_ID = '[A-Z]+(?:-[A-Z]+)*-\\d+[a-z]*';
    12	```
    13	
    14	**为什么是严格超集**：对旧式能匹配的任一输入，新式在同一起点吃掉的子串**逐字节相同**——
    15	`(?:-[A-Z]+)*` 与 `[a-z]*` 都能匹配空。已用本仓 store 实测（353/0/0，ID 集合逐个相同）。
    16	
    17	**为什么后缀这一段是必要的**：`leadId` 在正则之后还查边界
    18	（`next` 若是 `[A-Za-z0-9_]` 则作废），所以只放宽段数、不放宽后缀的话，`AC-30f` 仍然绑不上。
    19	
    20	---
    21	
    22	## D2 `lib/doctor.js` — 两处，互不相干
    23	
    24	### D2.1 D5 解耦（G2）
    25	
    26	```
    27	:33   const idRe = new RegExp(DEFAULT_ID);
    28	   →  const idRe = new RegExp(D5_TAP_ID);      // 模块内部常量，不导出
    29	      const D5_TAP_ID = '[A-Z]+-\\d+';         // 冻结：D5 判管道不判身份
    30	```
    31	
    32	**冻结值就是本 change 前的旧默认式**，因此 D5 的分类**逐字节不变**（AC-IP-09 由此**结构上必然**成立）。
    33	`:45` 的 `parsed` 算式**一字不动**——它那个「tagged SKIP/TODO 贡献 0」的既有怪癖不在本 change 范围（O8）。
    34	
    35	> 不导出这个常量，是为了让「D5 不是 `DEFAULT_ID` 的消费者」成为一条可静态断言的事实。
    36	
    37	### D2.2 D6 按类分流（G3）
    38	
    39	分类谓词（req-final §B2 已定死，此处只写成实现形状）：
    40	
    41	```
    42	firstToken(title) = title.trim().split(/\s+/)[0] ?? ''
    43	isIdShaped(tok)   = /\d/.test(tok) && /[-_]/.test(tok)
    44	```
    45	
    46	`collectScenarios` 返回的 `unidentified` 是 `[[file, title], …]`。按 `isIdShaped(firstToken(title))` 分两组：
    47	
    48	| 组 | 条数 | detail | fix |
    49	|---|---|---|---|
    50	| pattern-mismatch | 非空时**恰一条** | 含 `boundedSource(idp.source)` 与 `idp.origin`，样例取**前 3 个**、各截 40 字符 | 指向 `apriori/process-config.md` 的 `id-pattern` 行 |
    51	| missing-ID | 非空时**恰一条** | 沿用现状文案 | `add leading IDs`（**一字不改**） |
    52	
    53	`:148` 的重复 ID finding 与两者互不影响（AC-IP-13）。
    54	
    55	---
    56	
    57	## D3 `lib/archive-merge.js` — 一个新状态 + 一条新判定
    58	
    59	### D3.1 `IN_NOTES`（G4）
    60	
    61	`NOTES_LINE_RE = /^##\s+Notes\s*$/`，**必须在 `SECTION_LINE_RE` 与 `H2_LINE_RE` 之间**判：
    62	
    63	```
    64	:126   if (sec) { … }                          // 四种法定操作段
    65	       ← 在此插入：if (NOTES_LINE_RE.test(line)) { flush(); state='IN_NOTES'; kind=null; continue; }
    66	:127   if (H2_LINE_RE.test(line)) { … }        // 其它 h2 → problem + SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED
    67	```
    68	
    69	放这个位置就自动满足三件事：
    70	① `## Notes` 不再落进「未识别 h2」分支；
    71	② 它**不置** `sectionSeen`（`sectionSeen=true` 只在 `sec` 分支里）；
    72	③ 它能**终止** `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED`——因为 `:133` 的 `continue` 在这两条之后。
    73	
    74	`IN_NOTES` 的吞噬：紧接在 `:133` 之后加
    75	
    76	```
    77	if (state === 'IN_NOTES') continue;    // 段内一切不透明：Requirement / Scenario / 戳 / 自由文本
    78	```
    79	
    80	放在**戳处理之前**，才能保证「段内戳既不被采纳、也不报 problem」（req-final AC-IP-14d2）。
    81	围栏判定在 `:118-125`，本来就在最前，所以段内未闭合围栏照旧使其后不透明。
    82	
    83	**Notes 不是操作**：`deltaOpCount` 不动，Notes-only 的 delta 自然仍撞 `:375` / `:815` 的零操作拒绝。
    84	
    85	### D3.2 非-Requirement h3（G5）
    86	
    87	插在 `:166` 的 body 分支**之前**、`:145` 的 `REQ_LINE_RE` 分支**之后**：
    88	
    89	```
    90	if (state === 'IN_REQUIREMENT' && !blockDiscard && /^###\s/.test(line)) {
    91	  problems.push(`line ${n}: '${line.trim()}' is not a requirement heading — put explanatory
    92	                 content in a '## Notes' section`);
    93	  blockDiscard = true;                 // P2：作废当前块，停留在 IN_REQUIREMENT
    94	  continue;
    95	}
    96	```
    97	
    98	**三条不可动的边界**：
    99	
   100	| # | 约束 | 理由 |
   101	|---|---|---|
   102	| 1 | 必须在**戳处理（`:135-144`）之后** | 状态 A 保证「游离的戳绝不被当正文吸收」；放到戳之前会吞掉作废块内的戳，打破「RENAMED 逐字节不变」（r3 的教训） |
   103	| 2 | `!blockDiscard` 是判定的一部分（**P1**） | 已作废的块内不再产出第二条 problem；同时让**既有的** RENAMED 作废块行为一字不变 |
   104	| 3 | 只判 `IN_REQUIREMENT` | `FILE_PREAMBLE` / `IN_SECTION` / `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` / `IN_NOTES` 一律保持状态 A |
   105	
   106	`blockDiscard` 已经存在（`:158` 的 RENAMED 非法块、`:161` 的重名块都在用），本 change 只是多一个来源。
   107	
   108	---
   109	
   110	## D4 `templates/process-config.md`（G6）
   111	
   112	**三处**旧值都改：第 2 列 Value、第 4 列 Default、表下注释里的 `built-in default [A-Z]+-\d+`。
   113	只改第 4 列的话，`init` 写出的新项目会带着**旧** pattern 的 active config 行，
   114	把新 `DEFAULT_ID` 完全遮蔽，而静态文档验收却仍然通过（r1·REQ-2 的原话）。
   115	
   116	---
   117	
   118	## D5 外部共享状态的三个时刻
   119	
   120	本 change **不引入**任何外部共享状态，也不新增测试 seam：
   121	两个新常量是模块级不可变值，`IN_NOTES` 是解析期的局部状态。
   122	唯一「跨调用可见」的是 `apriori/process-config.md` 的模板内容——它由 `init` 一次性写出，
   123	之后归**人类**所有（R3），本 change 不改这个所有权。
   124	
   125	---
   126	
   127	## D6 测试策略要点
   128	
   129	| 面 | 手法 |
   130	|---|---|
   131	| 严格超集 | 对一批输入（含旧式能匹配的）逐个断言新旧两式返回**同一子串**；再对本仓 store 断言三个计数与 ID 集合相同 |
   132	| D5 解耦 | ① 构造 `ok 1 - AC-30f x # SKIP` 的 TAP，断言 D5 结论在默认式放宽前后**一致**；② 静态断言 `lib/doctor.js` 的 D5 路径不出现 `DEFAULT_ID` |
   133	| D6 分流 | 两类各构造若干场景，断言**恰两条** finding、各自只点名自己的场景、pattern-mismatch 的 detail 含 bounded source 与 origin、样例 ≤3 且各 ≤40 字符 |
   134	| `IN_NOTES` | 逐格走 req-final 的转移表：三个位置 × 重复 × 段内 Requirement/Scenario/戳 × 未闭合围栏 × 四种法定段的恢复 × Notes-only 零操作 |
   135	| h3 收紧 | `IN_REQUIREMENT` 一条 problem；作废块内第二个 h3 不刷屏；作废块内的**戳**仍按状态 A 处理（两种作废来源各一）；其余状态一字不变 |
   136	| 语料回归 | 既有的「每份归档 delta 零 problem」语料测试必须继续绿（实测语料中两种新构造各 0 处） |
   137	| 模板 | fresh `init` 后不手改 config，断言 `resolveIdPattern` 返回新 source、`origin: 'config'`，且 config 子进程认得三种 ID 形状（标题与 TAP description 两侧） |
     1	# proposal — id-pattern-and-delta-notes
     2	
     3	## WHY
     4	
     5	三件事，都是「**默认值与语法把合法输入判成了缺陷**」。
     6	
     7	1. **默认 id-pattern 认不出真实项目的 ID**。`[A-Z]+-\d+` 认不出 `AC-BIS-01`、`LIFE-DWS-01`、`AC-30f`。
     8	   棕地样本实测：151 个场景里 **50 个**绑不上——而那些 ID 完全合法（唯一、稳定、可被测试名携带）。
     9	   后果链：verify 报 GAPS → gate C1 BLOCKED → 每个 change 都要手写一段「这是结构局限不是缺陷」的免责说明。
    10	   4.1.0 把 pattern 变成可配置了，**但默认值没动**，而配置行住在人类持有的 `process-config.md` 里——
    11	   实测那个项目升到 4.1 后该文件仍是模板原样，**从没人加过那一行**。修复躺在二进制里，落地率 0。
    12	2. **doctor D6 的修复提示指错对象**。它说 `add leading IDs`，会把人打发去改 50 个合法 ID，
    13	   而正解是加一行 config（本 change 之后：什么都不用做）。
    14	3. **delta 语法里没有说明性内容的位置**。作者写的小节标题被当成指令而阻断归档；
    15	   更糟的是 **`### 非-Requirement 标题` 落在 requirement 块里会被静默并进 store**——
    16	   MODIFIED 路径上 4.1.0 的完整性报告能照出来，**ADDED 路径上照不出来**。
    17	
    18	## WHAT
    19	
    20	1. 默认式放宽为 `[A-Z]+(?:-[A-Z]+)*-\d+[a-z]*`——旧式的**严格超集**，对旧式能识别的每一个输入
    21	   返回**逐字节相同**的子串（本仓 store 实测：新旧两式的 identified/unidentified/duplicates 与 ID 集合完全相同）。
    22	2. **doctor D5 与项目的场景 ID 词汇表解耦**：它判的是 TAP **管道**，不是身份，却借用了 `DEFAULT_ID`。
    23	   不解耦的话，放宽默认式会让 `ok 1 - AC-30f x # SKIP` 这类行从 untagged 变成 tagged skip，
    24	   使 `parsed` 归零，把健康的 TAP 打成 `truncated or malformed`。
    25	3. **D6 按类分流**：首 token 含数字且含 `-`/`_` = ID 形状 → fix 指向 `id-pattern` 配置行；否则 → 维持原文案。
    26	4. **delta 新增 `## Notes` 不透明段**，并把「requirement 块内的非-Requirement h3」从静默并入改成带行号的 problem。
    27	
    28	## OUT OF SCOPE
    29	
    30	| 不做 | 理由 |
    31	|---|---|
    32	| 改 `resolveIdPattern` 的优先级（flag > config > 默认） | `gate-id-pattern` 刚立的契约，本 change 只换兜底值 |
    33	| 替用户往 `process-config.md` 写 `id-pattern` 行 | 违反 R3；而且本 change 的**要点**正是让大多数项目不需要那一行 |
    34	| 改 `leadId` 的边界规则 | 与 pattern 宽窄正交，改它会动每一个消费者的绑定语义 |
    35	| 让解析器「智能判断」哪些标题是说明 | 复盘明确列为过度方案。一个约定好的段名就够 |
    36	| 修 D5 把 tagged SKIP/TODO 算成 0 这个**既有**怪癖 | 它今天就存在（旧 pattern 认得的 ID 一样中招），与本 change 无因果；修它要改 D5 的分类语义，有自己的验收面。本 change 用**解耦**保证不把它变严重 |
    37	| 讨论模板该不该给 `id-pattern` 设 active Value | 独立的产品决定（要动 `init` 的模板哲学）；本 change 只保证两列 + 注释三处一致、不留旧值 |
    38	| change 2（`archive-preflight`）的任何内容 | 已按 owner 裁定挂起；本 change 只碰 delta **解析器**，与它改的高层 archive 块不是同一个 Requirement |
    39	
    40	## 必须让人类看见的三件事
    41	
    42	1. **默认值变更是外部可见的行为变更**：既有项目里此前 unidentified 的场景会变成已识别；
    43	   若其中两个恰好识别成同一 ID，会**新出现** duplicate 报错。这是期望行为——
    44	   重复 ID 本来就是缺陷，只是此前被 unidentified 掩盖。
    45	2. **`## Notes` 是新的语法面**，一旦发布就要长期兼容；段名与语义在本 change 里定死。
    46	3. **非-Requirement h3 由「静默并入」变「拒绝」是收紧**。本仓语料实测 0 处受影响，
    47	   但外部项目可能有——CHANGELOG 给出迁移建议（改成 `## Notes`，或降级为正文）。

 succeeded in 108ms:
     1	### Requirement: spec-runner binds scenarios to test runs
     2	`apriori verify` SHALL enumerate scenario IDs from the spec store, run the project's own test command, parse its TAP output, and report each scenario's bound/green state. It SHALL exit 0 iff every identified scenario is bound to at least one test and all its tests pass, with no orphans or unidentified scenarios.
     3	
     4	#### Scenario: SR-01 every scenario bound and green
     5	- WHEN every spec scenario ID appears in a passing test name
     6	- THEN each is reported BOUND-GREEN and the process exits 0 with "spec is the test suite"
     7	
     8	#### Scenario: SR-02 a scenario with no test is UNBOUND
     9	- WHEN a spec scenario ID appears in no test name
    10	- THEN it is reported UNBOUND and the process exits 1
    11	
    12	#### Scenario: SR-03 a failing test makes its scenario BOUND-RED
    13	- WHEN a scenario's test emits `not ok`
    14	- THEN the scenario is reported BOUND-RED (with pass/fail counts) and the process exits 1
    15	
    16	#### Scenario: SR-04 one scenario, many tests — green iff all pass
    17	- WHEN a scenario ID leads multiple test names and at least one emits `not ok`
    18	- THEN the scenario is BOUND-RED; it is BOUND-GREEN only when all its tests pass
    19	
    20	#### Scenario: SR-05 a test with no matching scenario is ORPHAN
    21	- WHEN a test name leads with an ID that no spec scenario carries
    22	- THEN it is reported ORPHAN and the process exits 1
    23	
    24	#### Scenario: SR-06 a scenario with no ID is UNIDENTIFIED
    25	- WHEN a `#### Scenario:` heading has no leading ID matching the id-pattern
    26	- THEN it is reported UNIDENTIFIED (unbindable) and the process exits 1
    27	
    28	#### Scenario: SR-07 delegates execution to the project's own test command
    29	- WHEN `--test-cmd` is given any command that emits TAP
    30	- THEN the runner runs it as-is and never assumes a language or framework (TAP is the only coupling)
    31	
    32	#### Scenario: SR-08 the id-pattern is configurable, default [A-Z]+-\d+
    33	- WHEN `--id-pattern` is omitted
    34	- THEN IDs are matched as `[A-Z]+-\d+`; when given, that pattern governs both spec and test extraction
    35	
    36	#### Scenario: SR-09 --json emits a machine-consumable verify report
    37	- WHEN `apriori verify … --json` runs
    38	- THEN it prints valid JSON — clean, result (GREEN/GAPS/ERROR), errors, exec (status/signal/error), duplicates, specFiles, boundGreen/boundRed (with pass/fail/skip counts), unbound, orphan, unidentified — and the exit code still encodes GREEN(0)/GAPS(1)/ERROR(2)
    39	
    40	#### Scenario: SR-10 zero parsed TAP results triggers a reporter hint
    41	- WHEN `apriori verify` runs a test command that produces output but not a single parseable TAP result line
    42	- THEN the report is preceded by a warning that the test command is probably not emitting TAP (naming node's `--test-reporter=tap`), so the all-UNBOUND list is not misread as missing tests
    43	
    44	#### Scenario: SR-11 execution status is part of the verdict
    45	- WHEN the test command fails to spawn, is killed by a signal, or exits non-zero while every parsed TAP result is green
    46	- THEN verify reports the run untrustworthy and exits 2 (never GREEN); a non-zero exit with matching red TAP remains ordinary GAPS (exit 1)
    47	
    48	#### Scenario: SR-12 vacuous inputs fail closed
    49	- WHEN a spec target does not exist, or the targets contain zero spec files, or the spec files contain zero scenarios
    50	- THEN verify reports the missing input and exits 2 — an empty universe is never a GREEN
    51	
    52	#### Scenario: SR-13 spec hygiene is enforced
    53	- WHEN the same scenario ID appears in more than one scenario heading, a scenario sits inside a markdown code fence, or a test/scenario name carries an ID followed by a word character — letter, digit or underscore (`XX-01b`, `XX-01_x`)
    54	- THEN duplicates are reported with their files and force GAPS; fenced scenarios are excluded as documentation; and the suffixed ID never binds to `XX-01` — it is unidentified/untagged instead of silently truncated
    55	
    56	#### Scenario: SR-14 TAP directives and aborts never count green
    57	- WHEN a TAP result carries a `# SKIP` or `# TODO` directive, or the output contains `Bail out!`
    58	- THEN skipped results count neither pass nor fail (a scenario with only skips stays UNBOUND), and a bailout makes the run an error (exit 2) regardless of earlier green lines
    59	
    60	#### Scenario: SR-15 --test-cmd falls back to the config
    61	- WHEN `apriori verify` is invoked without `--test-cmd` and `apriori/process-config.md` carries a `test-cmd` row (written by `apriori init --test-cmd`)
    62	- THEN that command is used; with neither present, usage exits 2
    63	
    64	### Requirement: projected verify binds against the candidate merged store
    65	`apriori verify --change <name>` SHALL construct the projection — the store as `apriori archive` would leave it after merging every delta spec under `apriori/changes/<name>/specs/` — in memory, never writing to the living store's location, and SHALL bind scenarios against that projection using the same `merge()` semantics archive uses. Discovery maps `changes/<name>/specs/<suffix>` to `apriori/specs/<suffix>` (`.md` files only); roots resolve against `--cwd` exactly as `apriori status` resolves change dirs.
    66	
    67	#### Scenario: SR-16 ADDED delta scenarios join the projection
    68	- WHEN a change carries an ADDED-only delta for a module and `verify --change <name>` runs
    69	- THEN the delta's scenarios join the projection alongside every existing store scenario — the CHANGE VERDICT demands the delta's scenarios while the untouched store scenarios' bindings report in the store report (change-scoped verify), with no duplicate-ID error from the overlay (genuinely duplicate IDs against a scoped scenario remain GAPS)
    70	
    71	#### Scenario: SR-17 MODIFIED delta replaces the demanded scenario set
    72	- WHEN a delta MODIFIES a requirement, changing its scenario set
    73	- THEN verification demands exactly the delta's version of that requirement's scenarios; scenarios the modification drops are not demanded
    74	
    75	#### Scenario: SR-18 REMOVED delta scenarios are not demanded and their tests orphan
    76	- WHEN a delta REMOVES a requirement whose scenarios have tests still tagged with their IDs
    77	- THEN the projection deprecates the block, its scenarios are not demanded, and each lingering test is reported ORPHAN in the store report — a PASSING lingering test no longer blocks the change verdict (a FAILING one still does unless a sibling change declares its ID)
    78	
    79	#### Scenario: SR-19 RENAMED delta demands the post-rename picture
    80	- WHEN a delta RENAMES a requirement Old → New
    81	- THEN verification demands exactly the projected picture — the block's scenarios under their unchanged IDs, with no demand arising from the pre-rename block
    82	
    83	#### Scenario: SR-20 merge conflicts make the projection untrustworthy
    84	- WHEN any module's merge reports one or more conflicts
    85	- THEN `verify --change` prints every conflict and exits 2 — it never verifies a partial or wrong projection
    86	
    87	#### Scenario: SR-21 projection inputs fail closed
    88	- WHEN the change name is invalid or fails realpath containment, the change dir does not exist, zero delta files are discovered, or any delta file violates a hygiene guard (empty/whitespace-only, content with zero operations, malformed or duplicated base stamp, duplicate requirement names)
    89	- THEN `verify --change` exits 2 with a message naming the offending path or file, and runs no tests
    90	
    91	#### Scenario: SR-22 --specs and --change are mutually exclusive
    92	- WHEN `apriori verify --change <name> --specs <dir>` is invoked
    93	- THEN it exits 2 explaining the projection defines the spec set
    94	
    95	#### Scenario: SR-23 --json carries the projection contract
    96	- WHEN `verify --change --json` runs — success, gaps, merge conflict, or any projection failure
    97	- THEN stdout is pure JSON in every class: the 3.0.1 fields plus `projection: {change, modules, conflicts}` where modules lists discovered store-relative suffixes (sorted) and conflicts carries merge-conflict strings verbatim; non-`--change` runs never emit a `projection` field
    98	
    99	#### Scenario: SR-24 a diverged base stamp blocks projection
   100	- WHEN a discovered delta file carries a base stamp that does not match the current fingerprint of its mapped store file
   101	- THEN `verify --change` exits 2 naming the store path and the expected vs actual fingerprint, and runs no tests
   102	
   103	### Requirement: deprecated blocks are excluded from verification
   104	In every verify form (plain `--specs` and `--change`), a requirement block whose heading line matches `/^###\s+Requirement:.*_deprecated \(superseded by [^)]*\)_/` SHALL be excluded from scenario collection: its scenarios are neither demanded nor listed, and a test still tagged with such a scenario's ID counts as ORPHAN. On stores containing no deprecated blocks, behavior is identical to 3.0.1.
   105	
   106	#### Scenario: SR-25 deprecated block scenarios stop being demanded
   107	- WHEN a store (or projection) contains a deprecated requirement block with scenarios, and a test still carries one of those scenario IDs
   108	- THEN those scenarios appear in no report group, the lingering test is ORPHAN, and all non-deprecated blocks are unaffected
   109	
   110	### Requirement: the TAP plan is a checked promise
   111	When the test command's stdout carries exactly one top-level TAP plan line (`/^1\.\.(\d+)\s*(#.*)?$/` — a trailing `# SKIP`/`# TODO` directive is legal and `N` still parses), the run SHALL be trusted only if the plan total equals the count of top-level TAP result tokens (unnumbered, undescribed, and dashless points count; `ok:`-prefixed diagnostic-like lines, top-level `pragma [+-]<word>` lines, and indented subtest/YAML lines never count) and no top-level test-point number repeats (compared numerically, so `01` duplicates `1`; unnumbered points are exempt). More than one top-level plan SHALL itself be an infra error naming the cure (one TAP stream per verify). A plan appearing MID-STREAM — with top-level test points both before and after it — SHALL be an infra error; a numbered test point OUTSIDE the plan's declared range (e.g. `ok 2` under `1..1`) SHALL be an infra error. Absent plans keep today's behavior — no promise made, none checked. Violations are infra errors (verify exits 2 / RESULT: ERROR; gate exits 2 / ERROR reporting the verify plan error — an untrustworthy run outranks a C1 binding block), never bindings; `verify`, `verify --change`, and `gate` inherit through `infraErrors` with no new wiring.
   112	
   113	#### Scenario: SR-26 a truncated plan refuses to verify
   114	- WHEN the test command emits a plan `1..2`, a single passing result, and exit 0
   115	- THEN verify reports RESULT: ERROR (exit 2) naming 2 declared vs 1 parsed, and nothing verifies GREEN
   116	
   117	#### Scenario: SR-27 duplicate test-point numbers are untrustworthy
   118	- WHEN one plan is present and two top-level results carry the same number (including `ok 01` vs `ok 1`)
   119	- THEN verify reports RESULT: ERROR naming the duplicated number(s)
   120	
   121	#### Scenario: SR-28 multiple plans fail closed even when totals mask
   122	- WHEN the output carries two top-level plans whose declared totals happen to equal the parsed points (`1..2`, one point, `1..1`, two points)
   123	- THEN verify reports RESULT: ERROR naming multiple plans and advising one TAP stream per verify
   124	
   125	#### Scenario: SR-29 the point count speaks TAP, not prefixes
   126	- WHEN a plan `1..2` is followed by a numbered described result and a bare `ok`, with an `ok: note` diagnostic line nearby
   127	- THEN the bare point counts toward the plan, the `ok:` line does not, and no plan error is raised
   128	
   129	#### Scenario: SR-30 plan-less, skip-all, and nested TAP stay legal
   130	- WHEN output has no plan at all, or a `1..0 # SKIP reason` plan with zero points, or node-style nested TAP whose indented subtest lines carry their own plans and results
   131	- THEN no plan infra error is raised and verification behaves exactly as before
   132	
   133	#### Scenario: SR-31 projected verify and gate inherit the plan check
   134	- WHEN a change's projected verify (or `gate --change`) runs against a test command whose TAP plan does not match its results
   135	- THEN `verify --change` exits 2 (RESULT: ERROR) and gate exits 2 reporting the verify plan error (an untrustworthy run is gate ERROR, like every other infra failure — not a mere BLOCKED)
   136	
   137	#### Scenario: SR-39 a mid-stream plan is untrustworthy
   138	- WHEN top-level test points appear both before and after a `1..N` plan line
   139	- THEN verify reports RESULT: ERROR naming the mid-stream plan — a plan promises the whole stream, not a suffix
   140	
   141	#### Scenario: SR-40 out-of-plan point numbers are untrustworthy
   142	- WHEN the plan is `1..1` and a top-level `ok 2` (or any numbered point outside 1..N) appears
   143	- THEN verify reports RESULT: ERROR naming the out-of-range number
   144	
   145	### Requirement: projected verify surfaces unstamped mutation deltas
   146	`verify --change` SHALL print one stderr warning per unstamped mutation delta (same message class as archive's) without affecting the verdict — an otherwise-GREEN run stays GREEN — and `--json` SHALL carry `projection.unstampedMutations` (the store-suffix-relative paths from buildProjection, `[]` when none; the field exists only where `projection` already does).
   147	
   148	#### Scenario: SR-32 the projection warns but does not judge
   149	- WHEN a change's delta carries mutation ops without a stamp
   150	- THEN verify --change warns on stderr naming the file and the stamp cure, the run can still be GREEN, --json carries projection.unstampedMutations with the suffix, and an ADDED-only unstamped delta yields an empty list and no warning
   151	
   152	### Requirement: unattributed test failures block GREEN
   153	Any top-level non-SKIP/TODO `not ok` SHALL block GREEN regardless of shape. Classification rides the shared TAP lexer: a column-0 point line whose remainder carries a directive (first UNESCAPED `#`, then a word starting with SKIP/TODO case-insensitively) is not a failure; a failing point whose DECODED description (escape table: `\\`→`\`, `\#`→`#`, other backslash pairs literal) leads with a scenario ID — dash-separated or dashless — stays attributed to its scenario; every other failing top-level point is an **unattributed failure**. Unattributed failures make the verdict GAPS (exit 1) even when the test command exits 0; they never downgrade an infra ERROR — when `run.errors` is non-empty both are reported and the exit is 2. `exec.status !== 0` SHALL never produce exit 0. The human report lists a new group (first 20 lines; a line longer than 120 chars is cut to 119 + `…` for a 120-char total; then `… and N more`); `--json` gains top-level `unattributedFailures: {count, lines[]}` with untruncated lines, present with a stable shape in EVERY outcome class (GREEN carries `{count: 0, lines: []}`); `parseTap`'s exported `untaggedFails` field stays present and compatible (doctor consumes it); untagged `ok` points block nothing and change no report/JSON shape beyond the new group.
   154	
   155	#### Scenario: SR-33 the teardown false-green is dead
   156	- WHEN the TAP stream is `ok 1 - XX-01 pass` / `not ok 2 - global teardown failed` / `1..2` and the test command exits 1 (the reviewer's reproduced input)
   157	- THEN the verdict is GAPS (exit 1) with the unattributed-failures group reporting 1 line — never GREEN, never exit 0
   158	
   159	#### Scenario: SR-34 bare and half-shaped not-ok points block even on exit 0
   160	- WHEN the stream carries a bare `not ok`, a number-only `not ok 3`, a dash-less `not ok 4 teardown`, or a number-less `not ok - teardown failed` at column 0 and the test command exits 0
   161	- THEN each counts as an unattributed failure and the verdict is GAPS (exit 1) — while bare or untagged `ok` points continue to block nothing
   162	
   163	#### Scenario: SR-35 directives, nesting, and prefix look-alikes stay exempt
   164	- WHEN a column-0 `not ok 5 # SKIP flaky` (or `# TODO`) appears, an indented `    not ok 1 - subtest detail` appears under a nested TAP block, and a diagnostic-prefix line like `not ok: summary` appears
   165	- THEN none counts as an unattributed failure: directives are exempt in any shape, indented lines remain nested-subtest detail, and non-point prefixes keep SR-29's shape discipline
   166	
   167	#### Scenario: SR-36 infra errors keep precedence
   168	- WHEN unattributed failures coexist with an infra error (a plan mismatch or a `Bail out!`)
   169	- THEN the run exits 2 with both the infra error and the unattributed-failures group reported — the new GAPS class never masks a fail-closed ERROR
   170	
   171	#### Scenario: SR-37 the reporting contract is exact
   172	- WHEN more than 20 unattributed failures occur, one of them longer than 120 characters, under `--json` and the human report
   173	- THEN the human report lists the first 20 lines (>120-char lines cut to 119 + `…`) and appends `… and N more`; the JSON carries `unattributedFailures.count` and the full untruncated `lines`; a GREEN run carries `{count: 0, lines: []}`; all other JSON fields keep their existing shapes
   174	
   175	#### Scenario: SR-38 gate C1 inherits the new GAPS class
   176	- WHEN `gate --change <name>` runs a test command whose TAP carries an unattributed failure
   177	- THEN C1 reports blocked with the verify gap counts — the false-green cannot re-enter through the gate
   178	
   179	### Requirement: TAP is a version-aware protocol
   180	The TAP stream SHALL be handled by a version-aware line lexer over **stdout only**. Line endings normalize first (CRLF and lone-CR both end a line). The version matrix is CLOSED: no version line, or `TAP version 12|13|14`, is accepted; any other numeric version (0..11, 15+) is an infra ERROR naming the version and the supported matrix; a column-0, YAML-outside line starting `TAP version ` that does not match exact digits is an infra ERROR; at most ONE version line is legal and it must precede every plan/point/bail-out — later or repeated version lines are infra ERRORs; indented or YAML-embedded version strings are diagnostics. Directive detection splits at the first UNESCAPED `#` (backslash escapes the next character); the description decodes `\\`→`\` and `\#`→`#` (other backslash pairs stay literal) before scenario-ID extraction, so `\#` is never a directive delimiter; directives are words STARTING with SKIP/TODO, case-insensitive (`# SKIPPED: platform` skips). `bail out!` matches case-insensitively at any indentation and aborts the run (infra ERROR). A top-level `---` opens a YAML diagnostic block whose lines never participate in any judgment; an unterminated top-level block is an infra ERROR naming its opening line. The ONLY ignored TAP-14 construct is a top-level line exactly shaped `pragma [+-]<word>` (never counted as a point). stderr is a separate diagnostics channel: `--json` carries top-level `stderr: string` in every outcome class (empty string when none, untruncated); the human report lists an `STDERR DIAGNOSTICS` group only when non-empty (first 20 lines, 119+`…` cap, `… and N more`); zero-TAP is judged on stdout alone and its hint names the `2>&1` remedy.
   181	
   182	#### Scenario: SR-41 the version matrix is closed
   183	- WHEN streams declare `TAP version 12`, `13`, `14`, no version, and each of `TAP version 0`, `9`, `11`, `15`, `99`, `banana` (column 0, outside YAML)
   184	- THEN the first four verify normally; every version in {0, 9, 11, 15, 99, banana} is RESULT: ERROR (exit 2) naming the version and the supported matrix — while an indented `    TAP version 99` AND a `TAP version 99` inside a closed top-level YAML block both stay diagnostics (TCSPEC-1)
   185	
   186	#### Scenario: SR-42 a version line is single and leading
   187	- WHEN a second `TAP version 13` line appears, or a version line appears after the first test point
   188	- THEN each is RESULT: ERROR — the version promise covers the whole stream
   189	
   190	#### Scenario: SR-43 escaped hashes are description, not directives
   191	- WHEN a TAP-14 stream carries `not ok 2 - XX-01 cleanup \# TODO is literal text` with exit 0 (the reviewer's reproduced false-green)
   192	- THEN the point is a REAL failure (bound red for XX-01), never a TODO skip; `ok 1 - XX-01 pass \# SKIP nope` stays a pass attributed to XX-01, its decoded description containing a literal `#`; and `not ok 3 - XX-01 x \\# SKIP later` IS a directive skip — the first backslash escapes the second, leaving the `#` unescaped (escape order, TCSPEC-2)
   193	
   194	#### Scenario: SR-44 bail-out is case-insensitive and indentation-blind
   195	- WHEN the stream carries `bAiL OuT! environment lost` (any casing, optionally indented)
   196	- THEN the run aborts as an infra ERROR exactly like `Bail out!` today
   197	
   198	#### Scenario: SR-45 lone-CR streams cannot hide failures
   199	- WHEN the whole TAP stream uses `\r` (lone CR) line endings and carries a failing point
   200	- THEN the failure is seen (GAPS/bound-red as its shape dictates) — line-ending style never swallows a line
   201	
   202	#### Scenario: SR-46 dashless descriptions bind and SKIP-suffixes skip
   203	- WHEN a stream carries `ok 1 XX-01 pass` (legal dashless description) and `not ok 2 - XX-01 cleanup # SKIPPED: platform`
   204	- THEN the first binds to XX-01 as a pass and the second is a directive skip — neither a false UNBOUND nor a false BOUND-RED
   205	
   206	#### Scenario: SR-47 stderr is a diagnostics channel with an exact contract
   207	- WHEN a logger writes `not ok 2 - noise` to STDERR while stdout carries a clean plan-matched stream; and a second run has empty stdout with TAP-shaped stderr
   208	- THEN the first run is GREEN with the stderr listed under STDERR DIAGNOSTICS (first 20 lines, 119+`…` cap) and `--json` `stderr` carrying it verbatim — the field present as a string in EVERY outcome class, empty string when silent; the second stays zero-TAP with a `2>&1` remedy in the hint
   209	
   210	#### Scenario: SR-48 YAML must close and pragma is the only pass
   211	- WHEN one stream opens a top-level `---` never closed before EOF; another carries `pragma +bail` between points under a matching plan
   212	- THEN the first is RESULT: ERROR naming the unterminated block\'s opening line; the second raises no plan error and no point-count drift (pragma lines are never points)
   213	
   214	### Requirement: the configured test command parses as structure
   215	`configTestCmd` SHALL read through the shared structured reader: fenced/commented `test-cmd` rows never take effect, and a `test-cmd` CONFLICT (two live rows, different values) is an infra ERROR (exit 2) naming the conflict — verify never silently picks a row; a missing row keeps today's usage-error path; gate inherits through verify.
   216	
   217	#### Scenario: SR-49 a conflicted test-cmd refuses to run
   218	- WHEN process-config carries two live `test-cmd` rows with different values (and, separately, only a fenced `test-cmd` row) and verify runs without --test-cmd
   219	- THEN the conflicted run is RESULT: ERROR (exit 2) naming the config conflict, and the fenced-only run behaves as if no test-cmd were configured (usage error) — never executing the fenced example
   220	
   221	### Requirement: the effective id-pattern resolves flag over config over default
   222	`verify` SHALL resolve its effective id-pattern in this order: the `--id-pattern` flag; else the `id-pattern` row of `apriori/process-config.md` (read through the shared structured reader, inheriting the full config-contract — fenced/commented rows inert, same-value duplicates tolerated, different-value rows a consumption-time CONFLICT); else `DEFAULT_ID`. When the flag is present the config key is NOT consumed — a broken or conflicting config row is invisible to a flagged run. The resolved source SHALL be validated (compiled) BEFORE any spec file is read and BEFORE the test command is spawned; an invalid effective pattern is an infra ERROR (exit 2) whose message names its origin — the flag message contains `--id-pattern`, the config message contains `process-config` — through the existing error contract (text mode `error:` lines + `RESULT: ERROR`; `--json` the existing ERROR shape with the message in `errors[]`). Flag presence is judged by PRESENCE, never truthiness: a present-but-empty `--id-pattern` is a flag-origin validation error (`empty --id-pattern`) and never falls back to the config. Pattern-error messages are sanitized AS A WHOLE — the assembled message (raw engine `e.message` never concatenated verbatim) is control-char-stripped and capped at 200 characters INCLUDING any ellipsis, so the raw source can never re-leak through the engine's own message. The two origins carry DIFFERENT trust contracts. The flag is operator-interactive input: compile validation only, matching runs in-process (documented trust assumption); `leadId`'s public semantics are untouched for every origin. A config-sourced pattern is repository input that CI consumes automatically, so EVERY actual application of it (scenario titles before the test command runs; TAP descriptions after) executes inside a terminable child process: a fixed child script shipped with the CLI (never source-interpolated code), spawned `shell:false` with the pattern and the text batch passed as data on stdin, answering on stdout, under a kill budget (SIGKILL). ANY child failure — timeout, spawn error, signal, non-zero exit, malformed output — fails closed through the config-origin sanitized error channel of the consuming command (verify/gate/check exit 2; doctor per its D6 finding rule); a title-batch failure aborts BEFORE the test command is spawned. On a `--change` run the error path still carries the existing `projection` JSON field (`{change, modules, conflicts, unstampedMutations}` with modules from delta discovery — a directory enumeration only; no spec content is read and no test command is spawned on the error path). The recognition contract itself is `leadId` semantics, shared by every consumer: the match starts at the title's first character, a following `[A-Za-z0-9_]` rejects the match, no `\b` is appended, the source compiles as written.
   223	
   224	#### Scenario: SR-50 the config row takes effect without a flag
   225	- WHEN `apriori/process-config.md` carries `| id-pattern | [A-Z]+(-[A-Z]+)*-\d+[a-z]* |` and `apriori verify --specs <dir>` runs without `--id-pattern` over scenarios `AC-01`, `AC-08a`, `AC-BIS-01`
   226	- THEN all three are identified (0 UNIDENTIFIED) — whereas the same run without the config row identifies only `AC-01` and reports 2 UNIDENTIFIED
   227	
   228	#### Scenario: SR-51 the flag overrides and shields the config
   229	- WHEN `--id-pattern` is passed while the config carries a different — or even a syntactically invalid — `id-pattern` row
   230	- THEN the run binds by the flag's pattern and reports no config error (the overridden key was never consumed)
   231	
   232	#### Scenario: SR-52 an invalid effective pattern refuses to run
   233	- WHEN the effective pattern comes from a flag whose source does not compile (or is empty), or from a config row whose source does not compile (no flag given) — including a `--change --json` run
   234	- THEN the run is an infra ERROR (exit 2), the message names `--id-pattern` (flag origin, empty included) or `process-config` (config origin), the WHOLE message is control-char-free and ≤200 chars including ellipsis (the raw source does not re-leak via the engine message — asserted on both origins), the test command is never spawned, no spec content is read, `--json` output keeps the existing ERROR shape with the message in `errors[]`, and a `--change --json` run still carries the `projection` field with its discovered `modules`
   235	
   236	#### Scenario: SR-54 catastrophic config matching is terminated, adversarial titles included
   237	- WHEN the config row carries a syntactically valid catastrophic pattern AND the spec titles are crafted to trigger its backtracking (both under the same repository's control) and verify runs without a flag
   238	- THEN the title-matching child is killed within its budget and the run is an infra ERROR (exit 2) whose sanitized message names `process-config` and the termination, with NO test command spawned; the same pattern via `--id-pattern` runs in-process (operator-trusted, documented); the project's real multi-segment pattern over the same store completes normally through the child
   239	
   240	#### Scenario: SR-55 every child failure class fails closed
   241	- WHEN the config-origin child runner (via its injectable test seam) is made to fail in each class — timeout, spawn error, signal, non-zero exit, and malformed output (non-JSON stdout; parseable JSON whose `ids` length differs from the input batch; an `ids` element that is neither string nor null)
   242	- THEN every class resolves to the same config-origin sanitized failure (verify: infra ERROR exit 2 naming `process-config`; the flag/default origins never touch the child), and a well-formed child response binds normally — the success shape is exactly one JSON document `{ids}` with `ids.length` equal to the batch length and each element `string|null`
   243	
   244	#### Scenario: SR-53 absent flag and config the default binds unchanged
   245	- WHEN neither a flag nor a config row exists
   246	- THEN the effective pattern is `DEFAULT_ID` and the run behaves exactly as before this change
   247	
   248	### Requirement: change-scoped verify separates the change verdict from the store report
   249	On a `--change` run, `verify` SHALL judge its verdict (exit 0/1) on the CHANGE SCOPE only, while reporting the whole projection informatively. The change scope is defined by requirement-block provenance over the projection: the blocks produced or renamed by the delta's ADDED / MODIFIED / RENAMED operations (idempotent reruns included; blocks whose final projected state is deprecated — including rename-then-remove — are excluded), with their scenario occurrences collected at occurrence level. `RENAMED` keeps its store semantics (requirement name only; scenario IDs preserved). The change verdict is GREEN iff every scoped scenario has ≥1 passing test and none red, no scoped occurrence is unidentified, and no scoped scenario ID occurs more than once ACROSS THE WHOLE PROJECTION (a cross-boundary collision is a binding ambiguity of this change; collisions entirely outside the scope go to the store report only). Out-of-scope PASSING tests are never ORPHAN for the verdict; a passing test whose leading ID exists nowhere in the projection is a true orphan, reported in the store report.
