### Requirement: apriori init scaffolds the workflow and per-tool pointers
`apriori init` SHALL scaffold the single `apriori/` root and write a thin pointer to the self-contained runbook in each selected AI tool's native location and format, interactively by default and non-interactively via flags, without ever overwriting existing files silently.

#### Scenario: IN-01 detects present tools and pre-selects them
- WHEN the project already contains a tool's marker (CLAUDE.md, .cursor/, .github/, …)
- THEN init pre-checks that tool in the multi-select

#### Scenario: IN-02 interactive arrow-key multi-select of tools
- WHEN run with no flags in a TTY
- THEN it presents an **arrow-key** multi-select (↑/↓ move, space toggle, a all, enter confirm — no numbered input) over {Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, GitHub Copilot, OpenCode, Windsurf}, detected tools pre-checked

#### Scenario: IN-03 non-interactive via flags
- WHEN run with `--tools a,b --test-cmd "…" --yes`
- THEN it scaffolds without prompting (CI-friendly)

#### Scenario: IN-04 the protocol is written once; tools get pointers
- WHEN any set of tools is selected
- THEN `apriori/runbook.md` is written once, **byte-identical to the package's own `RUNBOOK.md` (single source — no separate template copy to drift)**, and each tool gets only a pointer to it (no protocol duplication)

#### Scenario: IN-05 per-tool native location and format
- WHEN a tool is selected
- THEN its pointer lands at that tool's path in its format (e.g. Cursor → `.cursor/rules/apriori.mdc` with MDC frontmatter; Claude Code → `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/commands/apriori.md`; AGENTS.md shared by Codex/OpenCode)

#### Scenario: IN-06 additive and non-clobbering
- WHEN a target file already exists
- THEN init appends the pointer (rules files) or skips with a notice (runbook), never silently overwriting; re-running is safe

#### Scenario: IN-07 preview before writing
- WHEN about to write
- THEN it lists every file it will create or touch and asks to proceed (skippable with --yes)

#### Scenario: IN-08 reports command-level vs rule-level entry honestly
- WHEN a selected tool has no slash-command mechanism (e.g. Cursor, Copilot)
- THEN init states that tool gets a rule-level entry (point the agent at the runbook), not a `/apriori` command

#### Scenario: IN-09 --language pins a language in the scaffolded config
- WHEN init runs with `--language 中文` on a project without an existing config
- THEN the scaffolded `apriori/process-config.md` has its `language` field set to `中文` (default is `auto` = match the human); an existing config is never overwritten

#### Scenario: IN-10 the multi-select is arrow-key driven (no numbered input)
- WHEN the interactive selector runs
- THEN keys map as ↑/↓ = move (wrapping), space = toggle current, `a` = toggle all, enter = confirm, Ctrl-C/Esc = cancel; the rendered menu shows a cursor + `◉`/`◯` checkboxes, a `selected: <names>` footer (`(none)` when empty; names colored on a TTY), and never a numbered list; selection returns the chosen tool keys in order

#### Scenario: IN-11 a gitignored scratch dir for ephemeral instruments
- WHEN init scaffolds the `apriori/` root
- THEN it creates `apriori/tmp/` and an `apriori/.gitignore` containing `tmp/`, so P7 screenshot self-checks and similar ephemeral instruments never enter version control; an existing `.gitignore` is never overwritten

#### Scenario: IN-12 --test-cmd is persisted, not parsed-and-dropped
- WHEN `apriori init --test-cmd "<cmd>"` creates a fresh process-config
- THEN the config gains a `test-cmd` row that `apriori verify` uses as its default test command; an existing config is never rewritten

### Requirement: init records what it creates in the managed manifest
`apriori init` SHALL maintain `apriori/managed.json` entries ONLY for files it actually creates in a run: a fresh init records the runbook and each command file it wrote; init for an additional tool merges into a valid existing manifest preserving entries it didn't touch; a command file that already existed on disk is skipped as today and gains NO entry (existing content is never blind-adopted). When init creates a file that is absent on disk — including a manifest-listed file the user deleted as the prescribed cure — the entry is written/replaced with the hash of the bytes just written, so the next update sees `up-to-date`, not `modified`. `init --dry-run` never writes or modifies the manifest (it reports would-be entries). A hygiene-invalid manifest (per the update module's rules) makes init exit nonzero before scaffolding or merging anything.

#### Scenario: IN-13 fresh init writes the manifest for exactly what it created
- WHEN `apriori init --tools <t>` scaffolds a new project
- THEN `apriori/managed.json` lists the runbook and the created command file(s) with hashes of the written bytes, and nothing else

#### Scenario: IN-14 add-tool init merges without adopting bystanders
- WHEN init runs for an additional tool in a project where another tool's command path already carries a user file
- THEN the new tool's created file gains an entry, existing entries are preserved, and the pre-existing user file gains no entry (a later update reports it `unmanaged`)

#### Scenario: IN-15 the delete-and-reinit cure closes cleanly
- WHEN a managed file was locally modified, deleted, and `apriori init --tools <t>` recreates it
- THEN the manifest entry is refreshed to the recreated bytes and the next update reports `up-to-date`

#### Scenario: IN-16 init dry-run leaves the manifest alone
- WHEN `apriori init --dry-run` runs fresh or for an additional tool
- THEN the manifest is not created or changed, while the report shows the would-be entries

#### Scenario: IN-17 a hygiene-invalid manifest blocks init
- WHEN `apriori/managed.json` exists but is invalid per the hygiene rules
- THEN init exits nonzero naming the defect and writes nothing
#### Scenario: SR-48 YAML must close and pragma is the only pass
- WHEN one stream opens a top-level `---` never closed before EOF; another carries `pragma +bail` between points under a matching plan
- THEN the first is RESULT: ERROR naming the unterminated block\'s opening line; the second raises no plan error and no point-count drift (pragma lines are never points)

### Requirement: the configured test command parses as structure
`configTestCmd` SHALL read through the shared structured reader: fenced/commented `test-cmd` rows never take effect, and a `test-cmd` CONFLICT (two live rows, different values) is an infra ERROR (exit 2) naming the conflict — verify never silently picks a row; a missing row keeps today's usage-error path; gate inherits through verify.

#### Scenario: SR-49 a conflicted test-cmd refuses to run
- WHEN process-config carries two live `test-cmd` rows with different values (and, separately, only a fenced `test-cmd` row) and verify runs without --test-cmd
- THEN the conflicted run is RESULT: ERROR (exit 2) naming the config conflict, and the fenced-only run behaves as if no test-cmd were configured (usage error) — never executing the fenced example

### Requirement: the effective id-pattern resolves flag over config over default
`verify` SHALL resolve its effective id-pattern in this order: the `--id-pattern` flag; else the `id-pattern` row of `apriori/process-config.md` (read through the shared structured reader, inheriting the full config-contract — fenced/commented rows inert, same-value duplicates tolerated, different-value rows a consumption-time CONFLICT); else `DEFAULT_ID`. When the flag is present the config key is NOT consumed — a broken or conflicting config row is invisible to a flagged run. The resolved source SHALL be validated (compiled) BEFORE any spec file is read and BEFORE the test command is spawned; an invalid effective pattern is an infra ERROR (exit 2) whose message names its origin — the flag message contains `--id-pattern`, the config message contains `process-config` — through the existing error contract (text mode `error:` lines + `RESULT: ERROR`; `--json` the existing ERROR shape with the message in `errors[]`). Flag presence is judged by PRESENCE, never truthiness: a present-but-empty `--id-pattern` is a flag-origin validation error (`empty --id-pattern`) and never falls back to the config. Pattern-error messages are sanitized AS A WHOLE — the assembled message (raw engine `e.message` never concatenated verbatim) is control-char-stripped and capped at 200 characters INCLUDING any ellipsis, so the raw source can never re-leak through the engine's own message. The two origins carry DIFFERENT trust contracts. The flag is operator-interactive input: compile validation only, matching runs in-process (documented trust assumption); `leadId`'s public semantics are untouched for every origin. A config-sourced pattern is repository input that CI consumes automatically, so EVERY actual application of it (scenario titles before the test command runs; TAP descriptions after) executes inside a terminable child process: a fixed child script shipped with the CLI (never source-interpolated code), spawned `shell:false` with the pattern and the text batch passed as data on stdin, answering on stdout, under a kill budget (SIGKILL). ANY child failure — timeout, spawn error, signal, non-zero exit, malformed output — fails closed through the config-origin sanitized error channel of the consuming command (verify/gate/check exit 2; doctor per its D6 finding rule); a title-batch failure aborts BEFORE the test command is spawned. On a `--change` run the error path still carries the existing `projection` JSON field (`{change, modules, conflicts, unstampedMutations}` with modules from delta discovery — a directory enumeration only; no spec content is read and no test command is spawned on the error path). The recognition contract itself is `leadId` semantics, shared by every consumer: the match starts at the title's first character, a following `[A-Za-z0-9_]` rejects the match, no `\b` is appended, the source compiles as written.

#### Scenario: SR-50 the config row takes effect without a flag
- WHEN `apriori/process-config.md` carries `| id-pattern | [A-Z]+(-[A-Z]+)*-\d+[a-z]* |` and `apriori verify --specs <dir>` runs without `--id-pattern` over scenarios `AC-01`, `AC-08a`, `AC-BIS-01`
- THEN all three are identified (0 UNIDENTIFIED) — whereas the same run without the config row identifies only `AC-01` and reports 2 UNIDENTIFIED

#### Scenario: SR-51 the flag overrides and shields the config
- WHEN `--id-pattern` is passed while the config carries a different — or even a syntactically invalid — `id-pattern` row
- THEN the run binds by the flag's pattern and reports no config error (the overridden key was never consumed)

#### Scenario: SR-52 an invalid effective pattern refuses to run
- WHEN the effective pattern comes from a flag whose source does not compile (or is empty), or from a config row whose source does not compile (no flag given) — including a `--change --json` run
- THEN the run is an infra ERROR (exit 2), the message names `--id-pattern` (flag origin, empty included) or `process-config` (config origin), the WHOLE message is control-char-free and ≤200 chars including ellipsis (the raw source does not re-leak via the engine message — asserted on both origins), the test command is never spawned, no spec content is read, `--json` output keeps the existing ERROR shape with the message in `errors[]`, and a `--change --json` run still carries the `projection` field with its discovered `modules`

#### Scenario: SR-54 catastrophic config matching is terminated, adversarial titles included
- WHEN the config row carries a syntactically valid catastrophic pattern AND the spec titles are crafted to trigger its backtracking (both under the same repository's control) and verify runs without a flag
- THEN the title-matching child is killed within its budget and the run is an infra ERROR (exit 2) whose sanitized message names `process-config` and the termination, with NO test command spawned; the same pattern via `--id-pattern` runs in-process (operator-trusted, documented); the project's real multi-segment pattern over the same store completes normally through the child

#### Scenario: SR-55 every child failure class fails closed
- WHEN the config-origin child runner (via its injectable test seam) is made to fail in each class — timeout, spawn error, signal, non-zero exit, and malformed output (non-JSON stdout; parseable JSON whose `ids` length differs from the input batch; an `ids` element that is neither string nor null)
- THEN every class resolves to the same config-origin sanitized failure (verify: infra ERROR exit 2 naming `process-config`; the flag/default origins never touch the child), and a well-formed child response binds normally — the success shape is exactly one JSON document `{ids}` with `ids.length` equal to the batch length and each element `string|null`

#### Scenario: SR-53 absent flag and config the default binds unchanged
- WHEN neither a flag nor a config row exists
- THEN the effective pattern is `DEFAULT_ID` and the run behaves exactly as before this change

### Requirement: change-scoped verify separates the change verdict from the store report
On a `--change` run, `verify` SHALL judge its verdict (exit 0/1) on the CHANGE SCOPE only, while reporting the whole projection informatively. The change scope is defined by requirement-block provenance over the projection: the blocks produced or renamed by the delta's ADDED / MODIFIED / RENAMED operations (idempotent reruns included; blocks whose final projected state is deprecated — including rename-then-remove — are excluded), with their scenario occurrences collected at occurrence level. `RENAMED` keeps its store semantics (requirement name only; scenario IDs preserved). The change verdict is GREEN iff every scoped scenario has ≥1 passing test and none red, no scoped occurrence is unidentified, and no scoped scenario ID occurs more than once ACROSS THE WHOLE PROJECTION (a cross-boundary collision is a binding ambiguity of this change; collisions entirely outside the scope go to the store report only). Out-of-scope PASSING tests are never ORPHAN for the verdict; a passing test whose leading ID exists nowhere in the projection is a true orphan, reported in the store report. FAILURE SIGNALS stay fail-closed: a failure is non-blocking ONLY when it is provably attributable — bound to a projection scenario outside the change scope, OR carrying an ID declared as a scenario inside a SIBLING active change's cleanly-parsed delta (strict parse, zero problems, >0 operations; only ADDED/MODIFIED block bodies count — a parallel change's own red is that change's business; the sibling scan is part of the same single title batch; malformed, escaping, symlinked or unreadable sibling material grants NO exemption — skipping is the fail-closed direction; broken sibling material never INDEPENDENTLY creates an infra ERROR, and any failure left unattributed still blocks as GAPS); every `unattributedFailures` entry (no ID, no provenance — it may be this change's own test missing its ID) and every FAILING orphan whose ID appears in NEITHER the projection NOR any sibling delta blocks the change verdict exactly as today. The store report is a COMPLETE informative evaluation of the whole projection against the same TAP snapshot: `boundRed`, `unbound`, true-`orphan`, `unidentified`, `unattributedFailures`, `duplicates` (each a full list whose length is its count; `boundGreen` as a bare count) — out-of-scope reds, duplicates and unidentified MUST stay visible there. Execution follows a conditional single-run contract: invalid pattern → 0 projection builds / 0 content reads / 0 test spawns / 0 TAP parses (the fail-early order stands); projection or title-batch matcher failure → exactly 1 projection, 0 spawns; the normal and post-TAP-error paths → exactly 1 projection, 1 spawn, 1 parse; both views share the one projection and the one parsed TAP snapshot — the report never re-runs tests. Exit semantics: infra ERROR classes are unchanged; solely for `--change`, a non-zero test-process status that is EXPLAINED by parsed failures (`failCount > 0`) with no infra error no longer forces a non-zero exit — a clean change verdict is GREEN exit 0 (a scoped amendment to the non-zero rule; an unexplained non-zero stays ERROR exit 2). `--specs` runs are byte-identical to before.

#### Scenario: SR-56 the change verdict judges only the change scope
- WHEN the store has `R-A`(XA-01) and `R-B`(XB-01), the delta ADDs `R-C`(XC-01), and the test command prints `ok XC-01`, `ok XA-01`, `ok XZ-99` (a PASSING orphan), `not ok XA-01 again` (an out-of-scope bound red), then exits 1

## MODIFIED Requirements

### Requirement: spec-runner binds scenarios to test runs
`apriori verify` SHALL enumerate scenario IDs from the spec store, run the project's own test command, parse its TAP output, and report each scenario's bound/green state. It SHALL exit 0 iff every identified scenario is bound to at least one test and all its tests pass, with no orphans or unidentified scenarios.

#### Scenario: SR-01 every scenario bound and green
- WHEN every spec scenario ID appears in a passing test name
- THEN each is reported BOUND-GREEN and the process exits 0 with "spec is the test suite"

#### Scenario: SR-02 a scenario with no test is UNBOUND
- WHEN a spec scenario ID appears in no test name
- THEN it is reported UNBOUND and the process exits 1

#### Scenario: SR-03 a failing test makes its scenario BOUND-RED
- WHEN a scenario's test emits `not ok`
- THEN the scenario is reported BOUND-RED (with pass/fail counts) and the process exits 1

#### Scenario: SR-04 one scenario, many tests — green iff all pass
- WHEN a scenario ID leads multiple test names and at least one emits `not ok`
- THEN the scenario is BOUND-RED; it is BOUND-GREEN only when all its tests pass

#### Scenario: SR-05 a test with no matching scenario is ORPHAN
- WHEN a test name leads with an ID that no spec scenario carries
- THEN it is reported ORPHAN and the process exits 1

#### Scenario: SR-06 a scenario with no ID is UNIDENTIFIED
- WHEN a `#### Scenario:` heading has no leading ID matching the id-pattern
- THEN it is reported UNIDENTIFIED (unbindable) and the process exits 1

#### Scenario: SR-07 delegates execution to the project's own test command
- WHEN `--test-cmd` is given any command that emits TAP
- THEN the runner runs it as-is and never assumes a language or framework (TAP is the only coupling)

#### Scenario: SR-08 the id-pattern is configurable; the default recognises multi-segment and suffixed IDs
- WHEN `--id-pattern` is omitted
- THEN IDs are matched as `[A-Z]+(?:-[A-Z]+)*-\d+[a-z]*` — a STRICT SUPERSET of the historical `[A-Z]+-\d+` that returns the byte-identical substring for every input the old one matched, and additionally recognises multi-segment IDs (`AC-BIS-01`, `LIFE-DWS-01`) and lowercase-suffixed ones (`AC-30f`), which real projects use and which the narrow default left permanently unbindable; when given, the configured pattern governs both spec and test extraction

#### Scenario: SR-09 --json emits a machine-consumable verify report
- WHEN `apriori verify … --json` runs
- THEN it prints valid JSON — clean, result (GREEN/GAPS/ERROR), errors, exec (status/signal/error), duplicates, specFiles, boundGreen/boundRed (with pass/fail/skip counts), unbound, orphan, unidentified — and the exit code still encodes GREEN(0)/GAPS(1)/ERROR(2)

#### Scenario: SR-10 zero parsed TAP results triggers a reporter hint
- WHEN `apriori verify` runs a test command that produces output but not a single parseable TAP result line
- THEN the report is preceded by a warning that the test command is probably not emitting TAP (naming node's `--test-reporter=tap`), so the all-UNBOUND list is not misread as missing tests

#### Scenario: SR-11 execution status is part of the verdict
- WHEN the test command fails to spawn, is killed by a signal, or exits non-zero while every parsed TAP result is green
- THEN verify reports the run untrustworthy and exits 2 (never GREEN); a non-zero exit with matching red TAP remains ordinary GAPS (exit 1)

#### Scenario: SR-12 vacuous inputs fail closed
- WHEN a spec target does not exist, or the targets contain zero spec files, or the spec files contain zero scenarios
- THEN verify reports the missing input and exits 2 — an empty universe is never a GREEN

#### Scenario: SR-13 spec hygiene is enforced
- WHEN the same scenario ID appears in more than one scenario heading, a scenario sits inside a markdown code fence, or a test/scenario name carries an ID followed by a word character — letter, digit or underscore (`XX-01b`, `XX-01_x`)
- THEN duplicates are reported with their files and force GAPS; fenced scenarios are excluded as documentation; and the suffixed ID never binds to `XX-01` — it is unidentified/untagged instead of silently truncated

#### Scenario: SR-14 TAP directives and aborts never count green
- WHEN a TAP result carries a `# SKIP` or `# TODO` directive, or the output contains `Bail out!`
- THEN skipped results count neither pass nor fail (a scenario with only skips stays UNBOUND), and a bailout makes the run an error (exit 2) regardless of earlier green lines

#### Scenario: SR-15 --test-cmd falls back to the config
- WHEN `apriori verify` is invoked without `--test-cmd` and `apriori/process-config.md` carries a `test-cmd` row (written by `apriori init --test-cmd`)
- THEN that command is used; with neither present, usage exits 2
<!-- apriori-base: sha256:606fc080d3f86c6d689983ad3323c42ca95df4fa7a4873371e03e4ba5d9a8721 -->

## MODIFIED Requirements

### Requirement: the delta parser consumes its whole input
`parseDeltaStrict` SHALL be a sequential, fully-consuming parser: every line (outside code fences, which are opaque) belongs to exactly one legal construct — file preamble (stamp/blank/free text without structure markers), a legal `## ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED|RENAMED Requirements` heading, section-preamble free text, a `### Requirement:` block (body runs to the next heading or EOF), a RENAMED `- Old -> New` line, or a `## Notes` section (opaque commentary: everything under it up to the next fence-outside h2 is ignored entirely — never merged, never a problem) — and anything else is a `problems[]` entry carrying its 1-based line number. The exhaustive problem set: unrecognized h2 headings (one problem per heading; its lines are never absorbed into any bucket), requirement/scenario markers before any section, a requirement block inside RENAMED, a scenario marker outside any requirement, duplicate requirement names, A NON-`Requirement` h3 INSIDE A REQUIREMENT BLOCK, and the stamp problems (which also gain line numbers). Both `verify --change` (exit 2) and `archive` (exit 1) inherit the failure.

#### Scenario: AM-28 a misspelled section heading is reported, never absorbed
- WHEN a delta contains `## ADDDED Requirements` (or any other unrecognized h2) followed by requirement blocks
- THEN parsing reports the heading text with its line number, those requirements appear in NO bucket, and archive/verify fail closed

#### Scenario: AM-29 structure outside its legal home is reported with line numbers
- WHEN a `### Requirement:` appears before any section or inside a RENAMED section, or a `#### Scenario:` appears in a section preamble outside any requirement
- THEN each is a problem carrying its 1-based line number; multiple distinct problems are all reported

#### Scenario: AM-30 free text and fences stay legal; the corpus stays clean
- WHEN a delta carries titles/notes in the file preamble or section preambles, prose (`#####` and deeper headings included) in requirement bodies, or fenced content containing heading-like lines
- THEN no problem is reported — and every archived delta in this repo's `apriori/changes/archive/*/specs/` parses with zero problems (the regression corpus). An `###` heading that is not `### Requirement:` inside a requirement body is the ONE construct this list no longer covers: it is a problem, see the scenario below

#### Scenario: AM-31 stamp problems carry line numbers
- WHEN a delta carries a malformed, duplicated, or misplaced apriori-base stamp
- THEN each stamp problem names its 1-based line number

#### Scenario: AM-71 a non-Requirement h3 inside a block is a problem, not silent body text
- WHEN a `###` heading that is not `### Requirement:` appears inside a requirement block — the shape an author reaches for when explaining WHY a block changed
- THEN it is a problem carrying its line number and the delta is refused; today it is absorbed as body text and written verbatim into the living store, which the MODIFIED-integrity report can surface on a MODIFIED operation but never on an ADDED one, so the ADDED path corrupts the store silently
- AND the same heading in the file preamble, in a section preamble, or in the skipped region after an unrecognized h2 keeps its existing behaviour; `####` and deeper headings inside a block stay body text

#### Scenario: AM-72 one problem per bad block, and the stamp rule is untouched
- WHEN a non-Requirement h3 is followed by more prose and then a legal `### Requirement:`
- THEN exactly ONE problem is reported for that heading, the discarded block reaches no bucket, and the following legal requirement opens normally
- AND WHEN a stamp-shaped line appears inside a discarded block — whether discarded by this rule or by the pre-existing illegal-requirement-inside-RENAMED rule — THEN it is still handled by the stamp rules, never absorbed as body text: discarding suppresses only the new h3 check, never the parser's line-handling order

#### Scenario: AM-73 `## Notes` is opaque, and being opaque has consequences
- WHEN a delta carries one or more `## Notes` sections — before the first operation section, between sections, or at end of file
- THEN their content is ignored entirely: requirement and scenario markers inside them create no operations, a stamp-shaped line inside them is neither adopted nor reported, and no problem is raised; the section ends at the next fence-outside h2, where any of the four legal operation headings resumes parsing and any other h2 reports its own problem as before
- AND a legal stamp intended for the delta must sit BEFORE `## Notes`, since a stamp after it is inside it and therefore ignored — leaving the delta unstamped and subject to the existing CAS default-deny
- AND a delta consisting only of `## Notes` still fails the existing zero-operation guard: commentary is not an operation

     7	## D1 `lib/config.js` — 一个常量
     8	
     9	```
    10	:16   const DEFAULT_ID = '[A-Z]+-\\d+';
    11	   →  const DEFAULT_ID = '[A-Z]+(?:-[A-Z]+)*-\\d+[a-z]*';
    12	```
    13	
    14	**为什么是严格超集**：对旧式能匹配的任一输入，新式在同一起点吃掉的子串**逐字节相同**——
    15	`(?:-[A-Z]+)*` 与 `[a-z]*` 都能匹配空。已用本仓 store 实测（353/0/0，ID 集合逐个相同）。
    16	
    17	**为什么后缀这一段是必要的**：`leadId` 在正则之后还查边界
    18	（`next` 若是 `[A-Za-z0-9_]` 则作废），所以只放宽段数、不放宽后缀的话，`AC-30f` 仍然绑不上。

    57	## D3 `lib/archive-merge.js` — 一个新状态 + 一条新判定
    58	
    59	### D3.1 `IN_NOTES`（G4）
    60	
    61	`NOTES_LINE_RE = /^##\s+Notes\s*$/`，**必须在 `SECTION_LINE_RE` 与 `H2_LINE_RE` 之间**判：
    62	
    63	```
    64	:126   if (sec) { … }                          // 四种法定操作段
    65	       ← 在此插入：if (NOTES_LINE_RE.test(line)) { flush(); state='IN_NOTES'; kind=null; continue; }
    66	:127   if (H2_LINE_RE.test(line)) { … }        // 其它 h2 → problem + SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED
    67	```
    68	
    69	放这个位置就自动满足三件事：
    70	① `## Notes` 不再落进「未识别 h2」分支；
    71	② 它**不置** `sectionSeen`（`sectionSeen=true` 只在 `sec` 分支里）；
    72	③ 它能**终止** `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED`——因为 `:133` 的 `continue` 在这两条之后。
    73	
    74	`IN_NOTES` 的吞噬：紧接在 `:133` 之后加
    75	
    76	```
    77	if (state === 'IN_NOTES') continue;    // 段内一切不透明：Requirement / Scenario / 戳 / 自由文本
    78	```
    79	
    80	放在**戳处理之前**，才能保证「段内戳既不被采纳、也不报 problem」（req-final AC-IP-14d2）。
    81	围栏判定在 `:118-125`，本来就在最前，所以段内未闭合围栏照旧使其后不透明。
    82	
    83	**Notes 不是操作**：`deltaOpCount` 不动，Notes-only 的 delta 自然仍撞 `:375` / `:815` 的零操作拒绝。
    84	
    85	### D3.2 非-Requirement h3（G5）
    86	
    87	插在 `:166` 的 body 分支**之前**、`:145` 的 `REQ_LINE_RE` 分支**之后**：
    88	
    89	```
    90	if (state === 'IN_REQUIREMENT' && !blockDiscard && /^###\s/.test(line)) {
    91	  problems.push(`line ${n}: '${line.trim()}' is not a requirement heading — put explanatory
    92	                 content in a '## Notes' section`);
    93	  blockDiscard = true;                 // P2：作废当前块，停留在 IN_REQUIREMENT
    94	  continue;
    95	}
    96	```
    97	
    98	**三条不可动的边界**：
    99	
   100	| # | 约束 | 理由 |
   101	|---|---|---|
   102	| 1 | 必须在**戳处理（`:135-144`）之后** | 状态 A 保证「游离的戳绝不被当正文吸收」；放到戳之前会吞掉作废块内的戳，打破「RENAMED 逐字节不变」（r3 的教训） |
   103	| 2 | `!blockDiscard` 是判定的一部分（**P1**） | 已作废的块内不再产出第二条 problem；同时让**既有的** RENAMED 作废块行为一字不变 |
   104	| 3 | 只判 `IN_REQUIREMENT` | `FILE_PREAMBLE` / `IN_SECTION` / `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` / `IN_NOTES` 一律保持状态 A |
   105	
   106	`blockDiscard` 已经存在（`:158` 的 RENAMED 非法块、`:161` 的重名块都在用），本 change 只是多一个来源。

   137	| 模板 | fresh `init` 后不手改 config，断言 `resolveIdPattern` 返回新 source、`origin: 'config'`，且 config 子进程认得三种 ID 形状（标题与 TAP description 两侧） |

    56	- [ ] T35 AC-IP-19 归档 delta 语料仍逐个零 problem（既有语料测试保持绿）
    57	- [ ] T36 AC-IP-20 围栏内的 `###` / `##` 仍不透明
    58	
    59	### 模板与端到端
    60	- [ ] T37 AC-IP-21 `templates/process-config.md` 的 **Value 列 + Default 列 + 表下注释**三处都是新式（可 grep 断言旧式 `[A-Z]+-\d+` 在该文件中**零命中**）
    61	- [ ] T38 AC-IP-21b fresh `apriori init` 后不手改 config → `resolveIdPattern` 返回新 source、`origin: 'config'`；config 子进程对三种 ID 形状在**场景标题与 TAP description 两侧**都返回完整 ID
    62	- [ ] T39 AC-IP-21c 同一新项目上 check / doctor D6 / verify / gate C1 结论一致
    63	- [ ] T40 跑全量测试，记录失败清单（应大面积红）
    64	
    65	## I — 实现
    66	
    67	- [ ] I1 `lib/config.js:16`：`DEFAULT_ID` 换新式
    68	- [ ] I2 `lib/doctor.js`：新增**不导出**的 `D5_TAP_ID = '[A-Z]+-\d+'`，`:33` 改用它；`:45` 的算式一字不动

    72	- [ ] I6 `templates/process-config.md` 三处旧值
    73	- [ ] I7 全量测试到绿；`check --self` PASS；`verify --change id-pattern-and-delta-notes` GREEN

    80	- [ ] D4 逐条核对 AC-IP-01..24（含 14b..14g / 17b..17h / 21b..21c）全部有证据，对照表进 flow-state

    83	
    84	- [ ] S1 `apriori verify --change id-pattern-and-delta-notes` GREEN
    85	- [ ] S2 P8 异构一致性评审（R2，codex），verdict 落盘 + raw 存证
    86	- [ ] S3 ledger 全行终态
    87	
    88	（STEP6 的 KB 写回在 P9 阶段做——**含改正 `truth/doctor.md` 里那句「D5 的计数分类 pattern-insensitive」**，
    89	它已被 req-final §1.5 的反例推翻，与 change 1 修 "seven checks" 同类。）
     1	# proposal — id-pattern-and-delta-notes
     2	
     3	## WHY
     4	
     5	三件事，都是「**默认值与语法把合法输入判成了缺陷**」。
     6	
     7	1. **默认 id-pattern 认不出真实项目的 ID**。`[A-Z]+-\d+` 认不出 `AC-BIS-01`、`LIFE-DWS-01`、`AC-30f`。
     8	   棕地样本实测：151 个场景里 **50 个**绑不上——而那些 ID 完全合法（唯一、稳定、可被测试名携带）。
     9	   后果链：verify 报 GAPS → gate C1 BLOCKED → 每个 change 都要手写一段「这是结构局限不是缺陷」的免责说明。
    10	   4.1.0 把 pattern 变成可配置了，**但默认值没动**，而配置行住在人类持有的 `process-config.md` 里——
    11	   实测那个项目升到 4.1 后该文件仍是模板原样，**从没人加过那一行**。修复躺在二进制里，落地率 0。
    12	2. **doctor D6 的修复提示指错对象**。它说 `add leading IDs`，会把人打发去改 50 个合法 ID，
    13	   而正解是加一行 config（本 change 之后：什么都不用做）。
    14	3. **delta 语法里没有说明性内容的位置**。作者写的小节标题被当成指令而阻断归档；
    15	   更糟的是 **`### 非-Requirement 标题` 落在 requirement 块里会被静默并进 store**——
    16	   MODIFIED 路径上 4.1.0 的完整性报告能照出来，**ADDED 路径上照不出来**。
    17	
    18	## WHAT
    19	
    20	1. 默认式放宽为 `[A-Z]+(?:-[A-Z]+)*-\d+[a-z]*`——旧式的**严格超集**，对旧式能识别的每一个输入
    21	   返回**逐字节相同**的子串（本仓 store 实测：新旧两式的 identified/unidentified/duplicates 与 ID 集合完全相同）。
    22	2. **doctor D5 与项目的场景 ID 词汇表解耦**：它判的是 TAP **管道**，不是身份，却借用了 `DEFAULT_ID`。
    23	   不解耦的话，放宽默认式会让 `ok 1 - AC-30f x # SKIP` 这类行从 untagged 变成 tagged skip，
    24	   使 `parsed` 归零，把健康的 TAP 打成 `truncated or malformed`。
    25	3. **D6 按类分流**：首 token 含数字且含 `-`/`_` = ID 形状 → fix 指向 `id-pattern` 配置行；否则 → 维持原文案。
    26	4. **delta 新增 `## Notes` 不透明段**，并把「requirement 块内的非-Requirement h3」从静默并入改成带行号的 problem。
    27	
    28	## OUT OF SCOPE
    29	
    30	| 不做 | 理由 |
    31	|---|---|
    32	| 改 `resolveIdPattern` 的优先级（flag > config > 默认） | `gate-id-pattern` 刚立的契约，本 change 只换兜底值 |
    33	| 替用户往 `process-config.md` 写 `id-pattern` 行 | 违反 R3；而且本 change 的**要点**正是让大多数项目不需要那一行 |
    34	| 改 `leadId` 的边界规则 | 与 pattern 宽窄正交，改它会动每一个消费者的绑定语义 |
    35	| 让解析器「智能判断」哪些标题是说明 | 复盘明确列为过度方案。一个约定好的段名就够 |
    36	| 修 D5 把 tagged SKIP/TODO 算成 0 这个**既有**怪癖 | 它今天就存在（旧 pattern 认得的 ID 一样中招），与本 change 无因果；修它要改 D5 的分类语义，有自己的验收面。本 change 用**解耦**保证不把它变严重 |
    37	| 讨论模板该不该给 `id-pattern` 设 active Value | 独立的产品决定（要动 `init` 的模板哲学）；本 change 只保证两列 + 注释三处一致、不留旧值 |
    38	| change 2（`archive-preflight`）的任何内容 | 已按 owner 裁定挂起；本 change 只碰 delta **解析器**，与它改的高层 archive 块不是同一个 Requirement |
    39	
    40	## 必须让人类看见的三件事
    41	
    42	1. **默认值变更是外部可见的行为变更**：既有项目里此前 unidentified 的场景会变成已识别；
    43	   若其中两个恰好识别成同一 ID，会**新出现** duplicate 报错。这是期望行为——
    44	   重复 ID 本来就是缺陷，只是此前被 unidentified 掩盖。
    45	2. **`## Notes` 是新的语法面**，一旦发布就要长期兼容；段名与语义在本 change 里定死。
    46	3. **非-Requirement h3 由「静默并入」变「拒绝」是收紧**。本仓语料实测 0 处受影响，
    47	   但外部项目可能有——CHANGELOG 给出迁移建议（改成 `## Notes`，或降级为正文）。
    48	
    49	## 触及范围
    50	
    51	`lib/config.js`（一个常量）· `lib/doctor.js`（D5 分类 regex 解耦 + D6 fix 分流）·
    52	`lib/archive-merge.js`（`IN_NOTES` 状态 + 一条 h3 判定）· `templates/process-config.md`（三处旧值）。
    53	另：3 份 living spec、3 份 truth、RUNBOOK ×2、docs ×2、CHANGELOG。
     1	# req-final — id-pattern-and-delta-notes：默认值该认得真实项目的 ID，delta 该给说明留个位置
     2	
     3	> change: `id-pattern-and-delta-notes` · tier: large · track: harden
     4	> lineage: 分支 `brownfield-round2`（自 main@235a121 切出）；产品线 v4，最终目标 main；**禁止**合并到 v1 / v3
     5	> 证据来源：`apriori-in-practice/5.1/Apriori棕地项目使用复盘.md` §6 FP-1 / FP-2 / §8 P1-4，
     6	> 加本仓源码实证与两处**本次实测**
     7	> 本版处置 STEP0·r3 重开的 REQ-5（处置说明见 §七；REQ-1/2/3/4 已全部 verified）
     8	
     9	---
    10	
    11	## 一、问题（状态 A，全部可复现）
    12	
    13	本 change 打包三件互相独立、但共同指向「**默认值与语法把合法输入判成了缺陷**」的事。
    14	
    15	### 1.1 默认 id-pattern 认不出真实项目的 ID（复盘 FP-1）
    16	
    17	`lib/config.js:16`：
    18	
    19	```js
    20	const DEFAULT_ID = '[A-Z]+-\\d+';
    21	```
    22	
    23	它认不出**三段式**（`AC-BIS-01`、`LIFE-DWS-01`）与**带字母后缀**（`AC-30f`）的 ID。
    24	这类 ID 完全符合「可绑定 ID」的实质要求——唯一、稳定、可被测试名携带——只是不匹配这条正则。
    25	
    26	**为什么后缀也认不出**：`leadId`（`lib/spec-runner.js`）在正则匹配之后还查边界——
    27	
    28	```js
    29	const next = text.trim()[m[0].length];
    30	if (next !== undefined && /[A-Za-z0-9_]/.test(next)) return null;
    31	```
    32	
    33	所以 `AC-30f` 里即使 `AC-30` 匹配上，紧跟的 `f` 也会让它作废。
    34	
    35	**影响链**：verify 报 GAPS → gate C1 BLOCKED → 每个 change 都要在 flow-state 里手写一段
    36	「这是结构局限不是缺陷」的免责说明。
    37	
    38	**本次实测（棕地样本 `/mnt/d/agent-base/t_just-projects/apriori/specs`）**：
    39	共 **151** 个场景，按现行默认式有 **50** 个绑不上；换成 §二 提议的默认式后 **0** 个绑不上。
    40	
    41	**为什么 4.1.0 的修复没解决它**：`gate-id-pattern` 那个 change 把 id-pattern 变成了**项目可配置**，
    42	但**默认值一个字没动**，而配置行住在 `apriori/process-config.md`——一个 **R3 人类持有、agent 只读**的文件。
    43	实测那个棕地项目升到 4.1 之后，`process-config.md` **仍是模板原样**（只改了 language / cas / caps），
    44	`id-pattern` 行**从未被加过**。修复躺在二进制里，落地率为 **0**。
    45	
    46	### 1.2 doctor D6 的修复提示指向了错误的对象
    47	
    48	`lib/doctor.js:147`：
    49	
    50	```js
    51	d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding',
    52	  detail: `scenario(s) without a bindable ... ID${src}: ...`, fix: 'add leading IDs' });
    53	```
    54	
    55	在上面那个项目里，那 50 个 ID **完全合法**，问题在正则。
    56	按提示做，人会去改 50 个 ID；正确动作是加一行 config（或者，本 change 之后，什么都不用做）。
    57	**错误的 fix 提示比没有提示更贵。**
    58	
    59	### 1.3 delta 的语法里没有「说明性内容」的位置（复盘 FP-2）
    60	
    61	`lib/archive-merge.js` 的 delta 解析器（`parseDeltaStrict`）只认三种 h2 段
    62	（`## ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED|RENAMED Requirements`）。作者写的说明被当成指令解析：
    63	
    64	| 现象 | 位置 | 性质 |
    65	|---|---|---|
    66	| `## 场景 ID 逐条映射` 这类说明性小节 → `unrecognized section heading`，归档被阻断 | `:127` | **响亮**的误判——作者被迫降级标题或改写成段内附注（绕过而非表达） |
    67	| **`### 非-Requirement 标题` 落在 requirement 块内 → 被 `blockLines.push(line)` 当作正文并入，最终写进 store** | `:166` | **静默**的错误——比响亮的那个更危险 |
    68	
    69	第二条是我在读源码时自查发现的，复盘没提。它的形状是：
    70	
    71	```js
    72	:166  if (state === 'IN_REQUIREMENT') { blockLines.push(line); continue; }   // scenarios and prose are body
    73	```
    74	
    75	`### 说明` 不匹配 `REQ_LINE_RE`（`/^###\s+Requirement:\s+(.+?)\s*$/`），也不是 h2，
    76	于是在 `IN_REQUIREMENT` 状态下被当成正文，**原样并进 store 的 Requirement 块**。
    77	MODIFIED 路径上 4.1.0 的 MODIFIED INTEGRITY 报告能照出来（它逐行比对被替换的块），
    78	**ADDED 路径上照不出来**——没有旧块可比。
    79	
    80	### 1.4 状态 A 的其余事实（决定范围与风险）
    81	
    82	**`DEFAULT_ID` 的全部消费者**（本次 grep 实证）：
    83	
    84	| 消费者 | 位置 | 用途 |
    85	|---|---|---|
    86	| `resolveIdPattern` 的兜底 | `lib/config.js:131` | flag > config 行 > **DEFAULT_ID** |
    87	| `check` 的 CK-04 | `lib/check.js:163` `checkScenarioIds(specText, name, idPattern = DEFAULT_ID)` | 结构一致性检查 |
    88	| `doctor` 的 D5 探针 | `lib/doctor.js:33` `const idRe = new RegExp(DEFAULT_ID)` | TAP 解析。`truth/doctor.md` 称其 count 分类「pattern-insensitive」——**该声称已被 r1 用反例推翻**，见 §1.5 |
    89	| `doctor` 的 D6 | 经 `resolveIdPattern`（config > 默认） | 库健康 |
    90	| `spec-runner` 的 verify / gate C1 | 经 `resolveIdPattern` | 绑定门 |
    91	
    92	**本仓自身的 store 对默认值变更免疫（本次实测）**：
    93	
    94	| pattern | identified | unidentified | duplicates |
    95	|---|---|---|---|
    96	| 现行 `[A-Z]+-\d+` | 353 | 0 | 0 |
    97	| 提议 `[A-Z]+(?:-[A-Z]+)*-\d+[a-z]*` | 353 | 0 | 0 |
    98	
    99	且两者产出的 **ID 集合逐个相同**。本仓的既有测试因此不会因这条改动而变色。
   100	
   101	**delta 语料对语法变更免疫（本次实测）**：
   102	`apriori/changes/archive/*/specs/**/*.md` 里
   103	——非 `### Requirement:` 的 h3 共 **0** 处；非三种法定 h2 的段共 **0** 处。
   104	`apriori/specs/` 的 store 里非 `### Requirement:` 的 h3 也是 **0** 处。
   105	**没有任何存量产物需要迁移。**
   106	
   107	### 1.5 D5 的「pattern-insensitive」是假的（r1·REQ-1，已复核）
   108	
   109	`lib/doctor.js:45` 的计数是：
   110	
   111	```js
   112	const parsed = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0)
   113	             + untagged.length + unattributedFailures.length;
   114	```
   115	
   116	**被打了 tag 的 SKIP / TODO 贡献 0**（`r.pass` 与 `r.fail` 都是 0，且它不在 `untagged` 里）。于是：
   117	
   118	| TAP 行 | 旧 pattern | 新 pattern |
   119	|---|---|---|
   120	| `ok 1 - AC-30f pending # SKIP flaky` | `AC-30f` 绑不上 → 进 `untagged` → `parsed = 1` → **D5 ok** | 绑上了 → 只剩 `skip: 1` → `parsed = 0` → **D5 finding「truncated or malformed」** |
   121	
   122	`AC-BIS-01` 与 `not ok … # TODO` 有同样的反例。
   123	**所以「只换一个常量」会让某些合法的 TAP plumbing 从 HEALTHY 变成 FINDINGS**——
   124	而 AC-IP-09 恰恰要求它不变。两者不可兼得，必须在需求阶段裁掉一个。
   125	
   126	> 注意：这个计数怪癖**今天就存在**——`ok 1 - AC-01 x # SKIP` 这类**旧 pattern 也认得**的
   127	> tagged skip，今天同样会让 `parsed = 0`。本 change 并没有造出它，只是会让更多行落进那一格。
   128	
   129	---
   130	
   131	## 二、目标状态 B
   132	
   133	### B1 — 默认 id-pattern 放宽到认得真实世界的 ID 形状
   134	
   135	新默认值：
   136	
   137	```
   138	[A-Z]+(?:-[A-Z]+)*-\d+[a-z]*
   139	```
   140	
   141	- 是现行默认式的**严格超集**（`AC-01` 仍匹配，且匹配到的**子串完全相同**）；
   142	- 认得三段及以上（`AC-BIS-01`、`LIFE-DWS-01`）；
   143	- 认得字母后缀（`AC-30f`）——与 `leadId` 的边界规则相容（整个 ID 被吃掉，其后是空格）。
   144	
   145	**不改 `leadId` 的边界规则**（见 §五 O3）。
   146	
   147	### B1b — D5 与项目的场景 ID 词汇表**解耦**（r1·REQ-1 的处置）
   148	
   149	`doctor` 的 D5 只是在判「这条命令说不说 TAP」这个**管道**问题，它不关心项目的 ID 长什么样。
   150	它今天恰好借用了 `DEFAULT_ID`，于是被本 change 牵连。
   151	
   152	**处置：D5 不再是 `DEFAULT_ID` 的消费者**——它改用一个**属于自己的、冻结的**分类正则，
   153	其值就是本 change 之前的 `[A-Z]+-\d+`，并在源码与 KB 里写明理由：
   154	「D5 判的是管道，不是身份；它的分类刻意独立于项目的场景 ID 词汇表」。
   155	
   156	这样 AC-IP-09 从「一个未经验证的声称」变成**结构上必然成立**的事实：
   157	D5 根本不读那个常量，放宽它当然影响不到 D5。
   158	
   159	**不做的另一支**：把 D5 的计数改成基于 lexer 的 point/skip/todo 结构量。
   160	那会**改变 D5 今天的行为**（上面那条注记里的既有怪癖会被一并修掉），
   161	是一件独立的、有自己验收面的事——记入 O8。
   162	
   163	### B2 — doctor D6 按「ID 长什么样」给不同的修复提示
   164	
   165	D6 报 unidentified 时，必须先分辨这两类，给出**不同**的 fix：
   166	
   167	**分类契约（r1·REQ-3 的处置——「形似」必须有可执行定义）**：
   168	
   169	1. 取场景标题，`trim()` 之后按**第一个空白**切出**首 token**；
   170	2. 首 token 是「**ID 形状**」当且仅当：它**含至少一个数字** **且** 含至少一个 `-` 或 `_`。
   171	   （`AC-BIS-01` ✅ · `ac-01` ✅ · `AC_01` ✅ · `AC-30f` ✅ · `AC-01:` ✅（冒号不影响这两个条件）
   172	   · `123` ❌ 无分隔符 · `AC-` ❌ 无数字 · 空标题 ❌）
   173	3. 生效 matcher 对该标题返回 null 时：
   174	   - 首 token 是 ID 形状 → **pattern-mismatch** 类；
   175	   - 否则 → **missing-ID** 类。
   176	
   177	| 类 | fix 指向 |
   178	|---|---|
   179	| **missing-ID** | 「给场景加上前导 ID」——现状文案，**一字不改** |
   180	| **pattern-mismatch** | 「这些 ID 看起来是合法的，只是不匹配当前生效的 pattern；改 `apriori/process-config.md` 的 `id-pattern` 行」 |
   181	
   182	**detail 的输出契约**：pattern-mismatch 类的 detail 必须同时给出
   183	① 生效 pattern 的 **bounded source**（沿用既有的 `boundedSource`）与 ② 其 **origin**（`flag`/`config`/`default`）；
   184	样例最多 **3** 个，按**库内出现顺序**取前三，每个截断到 40 字符（沿用既有 D6 的截断惯例）。
   185	
   186	**finding 条数契约**：两类各产出**恰好一条** D6 finding（各自点名自己的场景），
   187	互不合并；`findings` 计数按既有规则 = finding 状态条目数，因此两类同时存在时比今天多一条。
   188	
   189	### B3 — delta 给说明性内容一个合法位置，并堵掉静默并入
   190	
   191	1. **一个被解析器忽略的 h2 段**：`## Notes`。它的整段内容不参与任何合并，也不产生 problem。
   192	
   193	   **`IN_NOTES` 的完整转移契约（r1·REQ-4 的处置）**：
   194	
   195	   | 问题 | 裁定 |
   196	   |---|---|
   197	   | 可以出现在哪里 | 第一个操作段**之前**、两个操作段**之间**、文件**末尾** —— 三处皆可 |
   198	   | 可以出现几次 | **不限次** |
   199	   | 进入时 | `flush()` 当前 requirement 块（与其它 h2 一致），状态转 `IN_NOTES` |
   200	   | 何时结束 | **围栏之外**的下一个 h2。若它是**四种**法定操作段之一（`ADDED` / `MODIFIED` / `REMOVED` / **`RENAMED`**——r2 勘误：v2 写成「三种」）→ 正常恢复解析；若是其它 h2 → 按 AC-IP-16 照旧报 problem |
   201	   | 段内的 `### Requirement:` / `#### Scenario:` | **全部忽略**，不产生任何合并操作、不产生 problem |
   202	   | 段内的 `<!-- apriori-base: … -->` | **全部忽略**：既不被采纳为 CAS 戳，也不因「重复戳」「戳在段后」而报 problem |
   203	   | Notes 标题本身是否置 `sectionSeen` | **否**——但这不代表戳可以跟在 Notes 后面，见下一行 |
   204	| 首个操作段**之前**的合法 CAS 戳该放哪 | **必须放在 `## Notes` 之前**。r2 指出 v2 自相矛盾：Notes 只在下一个 h2 处结束，所以「Notes 之后、首个操作段之前」的那个戳**仍在 Notes 内**，按「段内戳一律忽略」就该被忽略，而 AC-IP-14d 又要求它有效——两者不可兼得。裁定取**「Notes 真正不透明」**这一支：戳前置。（另一支是给 Notes 发明一个显式终止语法，那会扩大本 change 的语法面，不做） |
   205	   | 段内的代码围栏 | 照旧不透明；**未闭合的围栏**使其后内容保持不透明直到文件末尾（与既有 fence 语义一致） |
   206	   | Notes-only 的 delta（除 Notes 外零操作） | **仍按零操作拒绝** —— `deltaOpCount === 0` 的既有契约（`lib/archive-merge.js:375` / `:815`，D-AM-3）**一字不动** |
   207	2. **requirement 块内的非-`Requirement` h3 变成一个 problem**（带行号），不再静默并入 store。
   208	
   209	   **标题层级 × 状态矩阵（r1·REQ-5 的处置——只定义一个状态是不够的）**：
   210	
   211	   **r2 勘误**：v2 的矩阵把 `RENAMED` 当成一个 state，而它其实是**正交的 `kind`**；
   212	   而且漏了真实存在的 `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` 状态。于是「RENAMED 段内非法 Requirement 之后」
   213	   这个真实复合态（`state=IN_REQUIREMENT` + `kind=RENAMED` + `blockDiscard=true`）
   214	   会同时命中「`IN_REQUIREMENT` → 新 problem」和「`RENAMED` → 自由文本不变」两格，给出相反结果。
   215	
   216	   改为按**真实的 `state`**（外加两条优先级规则）表述：
   217	
   218	   | 行 | `FILE_PREAMBLE` | `IN_SECTION`（任一 kind 的段首自由文本） | `IN_REQUIREMENT` | `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` | `IN_NOTES` | 围栏内 |
   219	   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   220	   | `### Requirement: …` | 照旧 problem（块在段前） | 照旧开块；`kind === 'RENAMED'` 时照旧 problem + 作废块 | 照旧开新块 | 照旧被原 h2 的 problem 覆盖，静默跳过 | 忽略 | 不透明 |
   221	   | **其它 `### …`** | **照旧自由文本** | **照旧自由文本** | **新 problem**（受下方优先级 P1 约束） | 照旧静默跳过 | 忽略 | 不透明 |
   222	   | `#### Scenario: …` | 照旧 problem | 照旧 problem | 照旧正文 | 照旧静默跳过 | 忽略 | 不透明 |
   223	   | `##### …` 及更深 | 照旧自由文本 / 正文 | 同左 | 同左 | 照旧静默跳过 | 忽略 | 不透明 |
   224	   | `## Notes` | 进 `IN_NOTES` | 进 `IN_NOTES`（先 flush） | 进 `IN_NOTES`（先 flush） | **进 `IN_NOTES`**（h2 结束跳过态） | 重开一个 Notes 段 | 不透明 |
   225	
   226	   **两条优先级规则（必须钉死，否则 buckets 与 problems 会因实现选择而不同）**：
   227	
   228	   - **P1 —— `blockDiscard === true` 只优先于「新 h3」这一项检查**（r3 勘误）：
   229	     当前 block 已被作废时，**本 change 新增的那条 h3 判定不再触发**，该行按状态 A 落进 body 被静默吸收，
   230	     **不产出第二条 problem**。
   231	     **但 P1 绝不覆盖状态 A 在 body 分支之前的其它行处理**——尤其是 **CAS 戳**：
   232	     `STAMP_ATTEMPT_LINE_RE` 在状态 A 里于**每一个非跳过状态**都先于 `REQ_LINE_RE` 与 body 分支被匹配
   233	     （「一个游离的戳绝不被当作正文吸收」是它已有的保证）。v3 的「后续**所有**非-h2、非合法-Requirement
   234	     行继续静默吸收」会把戳一并吞掉，那正好破坏「RENAMED 逐字节不变」这条承诺。
   235	     **正确表述**：P1 是对**新增判定**的抑制，不是对状态 A 行处理顺序的改写。
   236	     这一条同时覆盖两个作废块来源：① 本 change 新增的「非-Requirement h3 作废块」；
   237	     ② 既有的「RENAMED 段内非法 Requirement 作废块」——后者行为因此**一字不变**。
   238	   - **P2 —— 出错后的恢复**：记录**一条** problem、置 `blockDiscard = true`、停留在 `IN_REQUIREMENT`，
   239	     直到下一个 `### Requirement:` 或任一 h2 才恢复。被作废的 block **不进** buckets。
   240	
   241	   **只收紧一格**：`IN_REQUIREMENT` 且 `blockDiscard === false` 时的非-`Requirement` h3。其余一律保持状态 A。
   242	3. RUNBOOK 的 delta 模板给出 `## Notes` 的示例。
   243	
   244	---
   245	
   246	## 三、验收标准
   247	
   248	### 默认 pattern（A 组）
   249	
   250	| ID | 若 | 则 |
   251	|---|---|---|
   252	| AC-IP-01 | 一个场景标题为 `AC-BIS-01 …` / `LIFE-DWS-01 …` / `AC-30f …`，在**未配置** `id-pattern` 的项目里跑 `verify` | 三者都被识别为已绑定 ID（今日全部 unidentified） |
   253	| AC-IP-02 | 现行默认式能识别的**任何**输入 | 新默认式识别出**逐字节相同**的 ID（严格超集，且不改变已有绑定） |
   254	| AC-IP-03 | 本仓自身的 store，分别用新旧默认式跑 `collectScenarios` | identified / unidentified / duplicates 三个计数与 **ID 集合**全部相同 |
   255	| AC-IP-04 | 一个标题没有任何前导 ID 的场景 | 仍为 unidentified（放宽不等于放弃） |
   256	| AC-IP-05 | 项目**配置了** `id-pattern` 行 | 配置仍然优先，默认值不参与——`resolveIdPattern` 的优先级（flag > config > 默认）一字不变 |
   257	| AC-IP-06 | 一个项目里同时存在 `AC-30` 与 `AC-30f` | 两者是**两个不同的 ID**，不构成重复 |
   258	| AC-IP-07 | 放宽后新出现的重复 ID（两个此前都 unidentified、现在都识别成同一 ID 的场景） | 照常报 duplicate——这是**期望的新失败**，须在 CHANGELOG 声明 |
   259	| AC-IP-08 | `check` 的 CK-04、`doctor` 的 D6、`verify`、`gate` C1 四个消费者 | 全部经由**同一个** `DEFAULT_ID`，无第二处硬编码 |
   260	| AC-IP-09 | `doctor` 的 D5 探针（`lib/doctor.js:33`） | 其 TAP 计数分类的结论**不因默认值放宽而改变**（`truth/doctor.md` 声称它 pattern-insensitive——本条把该声称变成断言） |

   271	### delta 语法（C 组）
   272	
   273	| ID | 若 | 则 |
   274	|---|---|---|
   275	| AC-IP-14 | 一个**其余部分合法且至少含一个操作**的 delta 含一个 `## Notes` 段 | `archive` 与 `verify --change` 正常通过；该段内容**不参与合并**、**不写进 store**、**不产生 problem** |
   276	| AC-IP-14b | 一个**只有** `## Notes`、没有任何操作的 delta | **仍按零操作拒绝**（既有 `parsed 0 delta operations` 文案不变）——Notes 不是操作 |
   277	| AC-IP-14c | `## Notes` 分别出现在首个操作段**之前** / 两个操作段**之间** / 文件**末尾**；以及出现**两次** | 四种都正常通过，段内内容一律不参与合并 |
   278	| AC-IP-14d | 合法 CAS 戳出现在 `## Notes` **之前** | 戳正常生效（Notes 标题不置 `sectionSeen`，因此不触发「戳在段后」的 problem） |
   279	| AC-IP-14d2 | 合法形状的 CAS 戳出现在 `## Notes` **段内** | 被**忽略**：既不采纳为戳，也不报 problem。若该 delta 有 mutation 操作且别处没有戳，它就是**未打戳**，按既有 CAS 默认拒绝路径处理 |
   280	| AC-IP-14g | `## Notes` 之后紧跟 `## RENAMED Requirements` | 正常恢复解析（四种法定操作段都恢复，不只三种） |
   281	| AC-IP-14e | `## Notes` 段结束于**围栏之外**的下一个 h2 | 若是法定操作段 → 正常恢复解析；若是其它 h2 → 照旧报 problem（AC-IP-16） |
   282	| AC-IP-14f | `## Notes` 段内含**未闭合**的代码围栏 | 其后内容保持不透明直到文件末尾，与既有 fence 语义一致 |
   283	| AC-IP-15 | `## Notes` 段内出现 `### Requirement:` / `#### Scenario:` / `<!-- apriori-base: … -->` | 全部被忽略（Notes 段是不透明的），不产生额外的合并操作、不打乱 CAS 戳的判定 |
   284	| AC-IP-16 | delta 含**其它**未识别的 h2（如 `## 场景 ID 逐条映射`） | 仍是带行号的 problem，文案与本 change 前一致——只有 `## Notes` 是新开的口子 |
   285	| AC-IP-17 | requirement 块内出现非-`Requirement` 的 h3（如 `### 说明`） | **成为带行号的 problem**，该 delta 被拒绝；今天它会被静默并入 store |
   286	| AC-IP-17b | 同上，且该 h3 之后还有若干行正文，再跟一个合法的 `### Requirement:` | 该 h3 只产出**一条** problem（不刷屏）；被作废的块**不进** buckets；其后的合法 Requirement 正常开新块 |
   287	| AC-IP-17c | 非-`Requirement` h3 出现在 `FILE_PREAMBLE` / `IN_SECTION` 段首自由文本处 | **行为与本 change 前一字不变**（自由文本） |
   288	| AC-IP-17e | `RENAMED` 段内先出现非法 `### Requirement:`（触发既有的作废块），其后再出现非-`Requirement` h3 | 由**优先级 P1** 裁定：静默吸收，**不产出第二条 problem**——RENAMED 段的既有行为一字不变（r2 勘误：v2 的矩阵在此给出相反结果） |
   289	| AC-IP-17f | 非-`Requirement` h3 出现在 `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` 状态（前面有个未识别 h2） | 照旧被那个 h2 的 problem 覆盖，静默跳过，不新增 problem |
   290	| AC-IP-17g | `## Notes` 出现在 `SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED` 状态 | 结束跳过态并进入 `IN_NOTES`（h2 是跳过态的终止条件） |
   291	| AC-IP-17h | **作废块内**出现一个 CAS 戳形状的行（两种作废来源各一：新增的 h3 作废块、既有的 RENAMED 非法-Requirement 作废块） | 该行仍按**状态 A** 的戳处理走（被采纳、或按重复/位置报 problem），**不被当作正文吸收**——P1 只抑制新增的 h3 判定，不改状态 A 的行处理顺序（r3 勘误） |
   292	| AC-IP-17d | `#####` 及更深的标题，出现在任一状态 | 行为与本 change 前一字不变 |
   293	| AC-IP-18 | 同上，但在 `ADDED` 段（无旧块可比） | 同样被拒绝——这正是 MODIFIED INTEGRITY 报告**照不到**的那一路 |
   294	| AC-IP-19 | `apriori/changes/archive/**` 的全部存量 delta 语料 | 逐个仍**逐字节等价**地解析（既有语料测试不得变色；实测语料中两种构造各 0 处） |
   295	| AC-IP-20 | 代码围栏内的 `### …` 与 `## …` | 仍然不透明，不受本 change 影响 |

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * spec-runner — bind spec scenario IDs to test runs, report per-scenario red/green.
     4	 * VISION endgame, weak form: "spec IS the test suite". Zero deps — pure Node stdlib.
     5	 * Coupling surface is tiny and language-agnostic: markdown scenarios + a TAP-emitting test command.
     6	 *
     7	 * FAIL-CLOSED: a deterministic gate must refuse to say GREEN when its inputs are missing,
     8	 * its instrument misbehaved, or its evidence is ambiguous. Vacuous success is a bug.
     9	 * Exit codes: 0 = GREEN · 1 = spec/test gaps · 2 = invocation or infrastructure error.

    11	const fs = require('fs');
    12	const path = require('path');
    13	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    14	// merge-family helpers shared with archive (3.1 projection); archive-merge never requires spec-runner

    17	// DEFAULT_ID lives in config (the zero-dep resolution home) and is re-exported here unchanged

    23	// Leading scenario/test ID. The match must end at a word boundary: `XX-01b` is NOT `XX-01` —
    24	// silently truncating would merge two different scenarios into one binding.
    25	function leadId(text, idRe) {
    26	  const m = text.trim().match(idRe);
    27	  if (!m || m.index !== 0) return null;
    28	  const next = text.trim()[m[0].length];
    29	  if (next !== undefined && /[A-Za-z0-9_]/.test(next)) return null;
    30	  return m[0];
    31	}
    32	
    33	function stripFences(text) { return text.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, ''); }
    34	
    35	// ---- the id matcher: one recognition contract, two execution channels -------------------
    36	// A config-sourced pattern is repository input that CI consumes automatically; its every
    37	// actual application runs inside a terminable child (SIGKILL on budget) so a catastrophic
    38	// pattern can never hang the CLI. Flag/default origins are operator/built-in trust: inline.
    39	const CHILD_PATH = path.join(__dirname, 'id-match-child.js');
    40	// the child's spawn contract, exported so tests assert the budget DETERMINISTICALLY
    41	// (a wall-clock window cannot prove the 2000ms figure)
    42	const CHILD_SPAWN_OPTS = Object.freeze({ timeout: 2000, killSignal: 'SIGKILL', shell: false });
    43	let childRunnerOverride = null;                 // test seam — SR-55 injects failures here
    44	function _setChildRunner(fn) { childRunnerOverride = fn; }
    45	function defaultChildRunner(payload) {
    46	  const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [CHILD_PATH], {
    47	    input: payload, ...CHILD_SPAWN_OPTS,
    48	    encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 8 * 1024 * 1024,
    49	  });
    50	  return { status: r.status, signal: r.signal || null, stdout: r.stdout || '', error: r.error || null };
    51	}
    52	// resolved = resolveIdPattern's success shape → { batch(texts) → {ids:(string|null)[]} | {failure} }
    53	function makeIdMatcher(resolved) {
    54	  if (resolved.origin !== 'config') {
    55	    const re = new RegExp(resolved.source);
    56	    return { batch: (texts) => ({ ids: texts.map((t) => leadId(t, re)) }) };
    57	  }
    58	  return { batch: (texts) => {
    59	    const runner = childRunnerOverride || defaultChildRunner;
    60	    let r;
    61	    try { r = runner(JSON.stringify({ pattern: resolved.source, texts })); }
    62	    catch (e) { return { failure: `spawn-error: ${String(e.message || e)}` }; }
    63	    // timeout FIRST: a real spawnSync timeout carries ETIMEDOUT and/or the kill signal —
    64	    // classifying by r.error first would misreport the termination as a spawn error
    65	    if ((r.error && r.error.code === 'ETIMEDOUT') || r.signal === 'SIGKILL')
    66	      return { failure: 'timeout (budget exceeded, child terminated)' };
    67	    if (r.error) return { failure: `spawn-error: ${String(r.error.message || r.error)}` };
    68	    if (r.signal) return { failure: `signal ${r.signal}` };
    69	    if (r.status !== 0) return { failure: `non-zero-exit (${r.status})` };
    70	    let parsed;
    71	    try { parsed = JSON.parse(r.stdout); } catch { return { failure: 'malformed-output (not JSON)' }; }
    72	    // strict success shape: exactly one JSON document whose ONLY key is a well-formed ids array
    73	    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed) ||
    74	        Object.keys(parsed).length !== 1 || !Array.isArray(parsed.ids) ||
    75	        parsed.ids.length !== texts.length ||
    76	        parsed.ids.some((x) => x !== null && typeof x !== 'string'))
    77	      return { failure: 'malformed-output (bad shape)' };
    78	    return { ids: parsed.ids };
    79	  } };
    80	}
    81	// the one sanitized failure text every consumer surfaces for a config-origin matcher failure
    82	function matcherFailureMsg(failure) {
    83	  return sanitizeMsg(`process-config id-pattern matching failed (${failure})`);
    84	}
    85	const inlineMatcher = (idRe) => makeIdMatcher({ source: idRe.source, origin: 'default' });
    86	
    87	function mdFiles(target) {
    88	  if (!fs.existsSync(target)) return [];
    89	  const st = fs.statSync(target);
    90	  if (st.isFile()) return [target];
    91	  const out = [];
    92	  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(target, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    93	    const p = path.join(target, e.name);
    94	    if (e.isDirectory()) out.push(...mdFiles(p));
    95	    else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) out.push(p);
    96	  }
    97	  return out.sort();
    98	}
    99	
   100	// Shared scenario scan over one text — STRUCTURE ONLY (no ID extraction; that is the
   101	// matcher's job so config-origin patterns stay inside the terminable channel).
   102	// Fenced content is documentation; scenarios inside DEPRECATED requirement blocks
   103	// (the marker REMOVED injects) are excluded in every form — they are neither demanded
   104	// nor listed, and their lingering tests become ORPHAN.
   105	function scanTitles(text, label, pairs, preClean) {
   106	  const clean = preClean ? text : am.stripDeprecatedBlocks(stripFences(text));
   107	  let m;
   108	  SCENARIO_RE.lastIndex = 0;
   109	  while ((m = SCENARIO_RE.exec(clean)) !== null) pairs.push([label, m[1]]);
   110	}
   111	
   112	// One batch over tagged pairs ([label,title,scoped]) plus sibling titles (appended to the
   113	// SAME batch — never a third matcher call) → { acc, ids, siblingIds } or { failure }.
   114	function makeThenBind(pairs, matcher, siblingTitles = [], integrityTitles = []) {
   115	  const titles = [...pairs.map((p) => p[1]), ...siblingTitles, ...integrityTitles];
   116	  const res = matcher.batch(titles);
   117	  if (res.failure) return { failure: res.failure };
   118	  const acc = { byId: new Map(), idFiles: new Map(), unidentified: [] };
   119	  pairs.forEach(([label, title], i) => {
   120	    const id = res.ids[i];
   121	    if (id) {
   122	      if (!acc.byId.has(id)) { acc.byId.set(id, []); acc.idFiles.set(id, []); }
   123	      acc.byId.get(id).push(title);
   124	      acc.idFiles.get(id).push(label);
   125	    } else acc.unidentified.push([label, title]);
   126	  });
   127	  const sibSlice = res.ids.slice(pairs.length, pairs.length + siblingTitles.length);
   128	  const siblingIds = new Set(sibSlice.filter((x) => x !== null));
   129	  const integrityIds = res.ids.slice(pairs.length + siblingTitles.length);
   130	  return { acc, ids: res.ids.slice(0, pairs.length), siblingIds, integrityIds };
   131	}
   132	
   133	// Scenario titles a SIBLING active change genuinely declares — an EXEMPTION source, so it
   134	// is strict: each sibling delta must parse cleanly (parseDeltaStrict, zero problems, >0 ops)
   135	// and only scenarios inside its ADDED/MODIFIED block bodies count (a rename-only sibling's
   136	// scenarios already live in this change's projection and bind as out-of-scope reds).
   137	// Anomalies — malformed deltas, symlinks, escaping paths, unreadable files — grant NOTHING:
   138	// skipping is the fail-closed direction here (fewer exemptions = a stricter verdict); they
   139	// are never an ERROR, or a broken sibling could block every other change's gate.
   140	// fault-tolerant .md walk for the sibling scan ONLY: every readdir/lstat failure at any
   141	// depth yields zero entries from that branch — an anomaly must never throw out of verify()
   142	// nor create an infra ERROR (it merely grants no exemption)
   143	function safeMdWalk(dir) {
   144	  const out = [];
   145	  let entries;
   146	  try { entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }); } catch { return out; }
   147	  for (const e of entries) {
   148	    const p = path.join(dir, e.name);
   149	    try {
   150	      if (e.isDirectory()) out.push(...safeMdWalk(p));
   151	      else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) out.push(p);
   152	    } catch { /* skip the branch */ }
   153	  }
   154	  return out.sort();
   155	}
   156	
   157	function collectSiblingTitles(cwd, change) {
   158	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   159	  const titles = [];
   160	  let entries = [];
   161	  try { entries = fs.readdirSync(changesDir, { withFileTypes: true }); } catch { return titles; }
   162	  for (const e of entries) {
   163	    if (!e.isDirectory() || e.name === change || e.name === 'archive') continue;
   164	    const siblingDir = path.join(changesDir, e.name);
   165	    if (!am.containsReal(changesDir, siblingDir)) continue;
   166	    const specsDir = path.join(siblingDir, 'specs');
   167	    let sd; try { sd = fs.lstatSync(specsDir); } catch { continue; }
   168	    if (sd.isSymbolicLink() || !sd.isDirectory() || !am.containsReal(siblingDir, specsDir)) continue;
   169	    for (const f of safeMdWalk(specsDir)) {
   170	      let st; try { st = fs.lstatSync(f); } catch { continue; }
   171	      if (st.isSymbolicLink() || !st.isFile() || !am.containsReal(specsDir, f)) continue;
   172	      let raw; try { raw = fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'); } catch { continue; }
   173	      const { delta, problems } = am.parseDeltaStrict(raw);
   174	      if (problems.length || am.deltaOpCount(delta) === 0) continue;   // malformed sibling grants nothing
   175	      const removed = new Set(delta.REMOVED.keys());
   176	      for (const bucket of [delta.ADDED, delta.MODIFIED])
   177	        for (const [name, block] of bucket) {
   178	          if (removed.has(name)) continue;
   179	          scanTitles(block, e.name, titles);
   180	        }
   181	    }
   182	  }
   183	  return titles.map(([, t]) => t);
   184	}
   185	
   186	// Batch-bind (label,title) pairs through a matcher → acc, or {failure} when the matcher fails.
   187	function bindPairs(pairs, matcher) {
   188	  const res = matcher.batch(pairs.map(([, title]) => title));
   189	  if (res.failure) return { failure: res.failure };
   190	  const acc = { byId: new Map(), idFiles: new Map(), unidentified: [] };
   191	  pairs.forEach(([label, title], i) => {
   192	    const id = res.ids[i];
   193	    if (id) {
   194	      if (!acc.byId.has(id)) { acc.byId.set(id, []); acc.idFiles.set(id, []); }
   195	      acc.byId.get(id).push(title);
   196	      acc.idFiles.get(id).push(label);
   197	    } else acc.unidentified.push([label, title]);
   198	  });
   199	  return acc;
   200	}
   201	
   202	function dupsOf(acc) {
   203	  return [...acc.byId.entries()].filter(([, titles]) => titles.length > 1)
   204	    .map(([id]) => ({ id, files: [...new Set(acc.idFiles.get(id))] }));
   205	}
   206	
   207	// Collect scenarios from spec files.
   208	// → { byId, unidentified:[[file,title]], files, missingTargets:[], duplicates:[{id,files}] }
   209	// (+ failure when the matcher's channel fails — callers fail closed on it)
   210	function collectScenarios(specTargets, idRe, matcher) {
   211	  const m = matcher || inlineMatcher(idRe);
   212	  const missingTargets = specTargets.filter((t) => !fs.existsSync(t));
   213	  const files = [...new Set(specTargets.flatMap(mdFiles))].sort();
   214	  const pairs = [];
   215	  for (const f of files) scanTitles(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'), f, pairs);
   216	  const acc = bindPairs(pairs, m);
   217	  if (acc.failure) return { failure: acc.failure, byId: new Map(), unidentified: [], files, missingTargets, duplicates: [] };
   218	  return { byId: acc.byId, unidentified: acc.unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates: dupsOf(acc) };
   219	}
   220	
     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * process-config parses as structure, never as full-text regex (config-contract).
     4	 * One shared reader for every consumer: fenced code blocks and HTML comments are
     5	 * non-content (unterminated blocks make the rest of the file inert — naturally
     6	 * fail-closed for waiver keys); a config row is any |-leading table row, first
     7	 * cell key, second cell value, extra columns ignored; same-key same-value
     8	 * duplicates are tolerated, different values are a CONFLICT problem. Problems
     9	 * surface only when the key is actually consumed.
    10	 */
    11	const fs = require('fs');
    12	const path = require('path');
    13	
    14	// The built-in scenario-ID pattern. Lives here (zero-dep module) so every consumer —
    15	// spec-runner, gate, check, doctor — resolves through one place; spec-runner re-exports it.
    16	const DEFAULT_ID = '[A-Z]+-\\d+';
    17	
    18	// Cell splitting honors the markdown pipe escape by backslash parity (CF-08/CF-09):
    19	// an odd run of backslashes before a pipe escapes it (the escaping backslash is removed,
    20	// the pipe joins the value); an even run keeps the pipe a separator, backslashes literal.
    21	// No other backslash sequence is ever unescaped — this is not a markdown renderer.
    22	function splitCells(line) {
    23	  const cells = [];
    24	  let cur = '', run = 0;
    25	  for (const ch of line) {
    26	    if (ch === '\\') { run++; cur += ch; continue; }
    27	    if (ch === '|') {
    28	      if (run % 2 === 1) { cur = cur.slice(0, -1) + '|'; }   // drop the escaping backslash, keep the pipe
    29	      else { cells.push(cur); cur = ''; }
    30	      run = 0;
    31	      continue;
    32	    }
    33	    run = 0;
    34	    cur += ch;
    35	  }
    36	  cells.push(cur);
    37	  return cells;
    38	}
    39	
    40	// parse the raw text → { values: Map<key,value>, conflicts: Set<key> }
    41	function parseConfig(text) {
    42	  const values = new Map();
    43	  const conflicts = new Set();
    44	  let fence = false, comment = false;
    45	  for (const raw of (text || '').split('\n')) {
    46	    const line = raw.endsWith('\r') ? raw.slice(0, -1) : raw;
    47	    let t = line;
    48	    if (comment) {
    49	      const close = t.indexOf('-->');
    50	      if (close < 0) continue;
    51	      comment = false;
    52	      t = t.slice(close + 3);
    53	    }
    54	    if (fence) { if (/^\s*```/.test(t)) fence = false; continue; }
    55	    if (/^\s*```/.test(t)) { fence = true; continue; }
    56	    // strip inline comment SPANS — content before the opener stays live
    57	    while (t.includes('<!--')) {
    58	      const open = t.indexOf('<!--');
    59	      const close = t.indexOf('-->', open + 4);
    60	      if (close < 0) { t = t.slice(0, open); comment = true; break; }
    61	      t = t.slice(0, open) + t.slice(close + 3);
    62	    }
    63	    if (!t.trimStart().startsWith('|')) continue;
    64	    const cells = splitCells(t).map((c) => c.trim());
    65	    // cells[0] is the empty prefix before the leading pipe
    66	    const key = cells[1], value = cells[2];
    67	    if (!key || value === undefined || value === '') continue;
    68	    if (key.toLowerCase() === 'key' || /^-+$/.test(key)) continue;      // header / separator
    69	    if (/^-+$/.test(value)) continue;
    70	    if (values.has(key) && values.get(key) !== value) { conflicts.add(key); continue; }
    71	    values.set(key, value);
    72	  }
    73	  return { values, conflicts };
    74	}
    75	
    76	function readConfig(cwd) {
    77	  const p = path.join(cwd || '.', 'apriori', 'process-config.md');
    78	  // read first, classify after: ONLY a definite ENOENT is absence — every other failure
    79	  // (directory, permissions, unreachable ancestor, …) is an unreadable-config problem,
    80	  // never a silent fall-through to defaults (an existsSync pre-check would misreport a
    81	  // permission-blocked file as absent)
    82	  let text;
    83	  try { text = fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'); }
    84	  catch (e) {
    85	    if (e.code === 'ENOENT') {
    86	      // ENOENT alone does not prove absence: a dangling symlink stats as an existing
    87	      // directory ENTRY whose read fails — that is present-but-unreadable, never absent
    88	      let present = false;
    89	      try { fs.lstatSync(p); present = true; } catch { /* truly absent */ }
    90	      if (!present) return { values: new Map(), conflicts: new Set() };
    91	    }
    92	    return { values: new Map(), conflicts: new Set(), unreadable: String(e.code || e.message) };
    93	  }
    94	  return parseConfig(text);
    95	}
    96	
    97	// one key → { value: string|null, problem: string|null } (problem only when consumed)
    98	function getConfig(cwd, key) {
    99	  const { values, conflicts, unreadable } = readConfig(cwd);
   100	  if (unreadable) return { value: null, problem: `apriori/process-config.md exists but cannot be read (${unreadable}) — fix the file` };
   101	  if (conflicts.has(key)) return { value: null, problem: `process-config carries conflicting '${key}' rows — resolve the conflict (one live row per key)` };
   102	  return { value: values.has(key) ? values.get(key) : null, problem: null };
   103	}
   104	
   105	// Message sanitization is the single exit for pattern errors: control chars stripped,
   106	// total length capped INCLUDING the ellipsis; the engine's e.message is never concatenated.
   107	function sanitizeMsg(msg) {
   108	  const clean = msg.replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/g, '·');
   109	  return clean.length > 200 ? clean.slice(0, 199) + '…' : clean;
   110	}
   111	function boundedSource(s) {
   112	  const clean = String(s).replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/g, '·');
   113	  return clean.length > 80 ? clean.slice(0, 79) + '…' : clean;
   114	}
   115	
   116	// Effective id-pattern: flag (by PRESENCE — an empty flag is a flag-origin error, never a
   117	// config fallback) > config `id-pattern` row > DEFAULT_ID.
   118	// → { source, origin: 'flag'|'config'|'default' } | { error }
   119	function resolveIdPattern(cwd, flagValue) {
   120	  if (flagValue !== null && flagValue !== undefined) {
   121	    if (flagValue === '') return { error: 'empty --id-pattern' };
   122	    try { new RegExp(flagValue); } catch { return { error: sanitizeMsg(`invalid --id-pattern '${boundedSource(flagValue)}' (regex does not compile)`) }; }
   123	    return { source: flagValue, origin: 'flag' };
   124	  }
   125	  const { value, problem } = getConfig(cwd, 'id-pattern');
   126	  if (problem) return { error: problem };
   127	  if (value !== null) {
   128	    try { new RegExp(value); } catch { return { error: sanitizeMsg(`process-config id-pattern row is invalid: '${boundedSource(value)}' (regex does not compile)`) }; }
   129	    return { source: value, origin: 'config' };
   130	  }
   131	  return { source: DEFAULT_ID, origin: 'default' };
   132	}
   133	
   134	// Effective verification-profile: the row is human-owned and OPT-IN. An absent row, an empty
   135	// value cell (the shared parser skips empty-valued rows for every key) and an explicit `none`
   136	// all mean "nothing escalates" — never a default value. An out-of-set non-empty value is a
   137	// consumption-time problem, never a silent fallback.
   138	// → { profile, origin: 'config'|'absent'|'none', problem }
   139	const VERIFICATION_PROFILES = ['ui', 'backend', 'fullstack', 'docs', 'none'];
   140	function resolveVerificationProfile(cwd) {
   141	  const { value, problem } = getConfig(cwd, 'verification-profile');
   142	  if (problem) return { profile: null, origin: null, problem };
   143	  if (value === null) return { profile: null, origin: 'absent', problem: null };
   144	  const v = value.trim();
   145	  if (v === 'none') return { profile: null, origin: 'none', problem: null };
   146	  if (!VERIFICATION_PROFILES.includes(v)) {
   147	    return {
   148	      profile: null,
   149	      origin: null,
   150	      problem: sanitizeMsg(`process-config verification-profile row is invalid: '${boundedSource(v)}' (legal: ${VERIFICATION_PROFILES.join(', ')})`),
   151	    };
   152	  }
   153	  return { profile: v, origin: 'config', problem: null };
   154	}
   155	
   156	module.exports = { parseConfig, readConfig, getConfig, splitCells, DEFAULT_ID, resolveIdPattern, sanitizeMsg, boundedSource, resolveVerificationProfile, VERIFICATION_PROFILES };
   140	}
   141	
   142	// ---- CK-03 (checker 8): codex known-good forms (ported from v2) --------------
   143	const CODEX_EXEMPT = [['codex', 'exec', 'resume', '-s', 'read-only', '<session-id>']];
   144	function checkCodexKnownForms(files) {
   145	  const fails = [];
   146	  for (const [name, text] of Object.entries(files)) {
   147	    for (const tokens of codexCandidates(text)) {
   148	      if (!tokens.includes('resume')) continue;
   149	      if (CODEX_EXEMPT.some((e) => e.join(' ') === tokens.join(' '))) continue;
   150	      if (tokens.includes('-s')) fails.push(`${name}: resume command uses -s (rejected on codex >=0.14x): ${tokens.join(' ')}`);
   151	      if (!(tokens.includes('-c') && tokens.includes('sandbox_mode="read-only"')))
   152	        fails.push(`${name}: resume command missing -c sandbox_mode="read-only": ${tokens.join(' ')}`);
   153	    }
   154	  }
   155	  for (const rb of ['RUNBOOK.md', 'RUNBOOK_cn.md'])
   156	    if (files[rb] && !files[rb].includes('< /dev/null')) fails.push(`${rb}: missing the \`< /dev/null\` non-interactive guidance`);
   157	  return fails;
   158	}
   159	
   160	// ---- CK-04: every spec scenario carries a bindable ID -----------------------
   161	// Recognition goes through leadId — the same contract verify/gate/doctor use — never a
   162	// private `^(…)\b` anchoring that could disagree with them at the edges (CK-14).
   163	function checkScenarioIds(specText, name, idPattern = DEFAULT_ID) {
   164	  const idRe = new RegExp(idPattern);
   165	  const fails = [];
   166	  // fenced scenarios are documentation, not spec — same rule as the spec-runner (SR-13)
   167	  for (const m of stripFences(specText).matchAll(/^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/gm))
   168	    if (leadId(m[1].trim(), idRe) === null) fails.push(`${name}: scenario without a bindable ID: ${m[1].slice(0, 50)}`);
   169	  return fails;
   170	}
   171	
   172	// scenario titles of one spec text, in order (fence rule as above) — the CLI batches
   173	// these through the shared matcher so config-origin patterns stay terminable (CK-16)
   174	function scenarioTitles(specText) {
   175	  return [...stripFences(specText).matchAll(/^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/gm)].map((m) => m[1]);
   176	}
   177	
   178	// ---- CK-05: no OpenSpec adapter references remain (V3 is single plain-files) --
   179	const OPENSPEC_MARKERS = [/\/opsx:/, /openspec\//, /\(adapter:/i, /OpenSpec adapter/];
   180	function checkNoOpenspec(name, text) {
   181	  const fails = [];
   182	  for (const line of text.split('\n')) for (const re of OPENSPEC_MARKERS)
   183	    if (re.test(line)) { fails.push(`${name}: residual OpenSpec reference: ${line.trim().slice(0, 60)}`); break; }
   184	  return fails;
   185	}

   187	// ---- CK-10: review-evidence secret tripwire (consumer mode) -------------------
   188	// Three literal formats ONLY — each with an airtight no-legit-match argument. This is a
   189	// tripwire, not a DLP product: fuzzy patterns false-positive on code discussion, which
   190	// trains people to ignore the tripwire. Per-line SUBSTRING scan (secrets embed in output).
     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * apriori doctor — diagnose the project↔apriori seam. Seven checks, one exit code:
     4	 * 0 = HEALTHY · 1 = FINDINGS · 2 = UNUSABLE (Node floor, uninitialized, usage error).
     5	 * Doctor never repairs and never writes; its ONLY side effect is D5 running the
     6	 * project's test command once (--no-run removes even that). Findings name their fixer.
     7	 */
     8	const fs = require('fs');
     9	const path = require('path');
    10	const { TOOLS, detectTools } = require('./init');
    11	const { checkRunbookFreshness } = require('./check');
    12	const { collectScenarios, parseTap, zeroTapParsed, runTestCommand, configTestCmd, DEFAULT_ID, looksLikeTap, makeIdMatcher, matcherFailureMsg } = require('./spec-runner');
    13	const { resolveIdPattern, boundedSource } = require('./config');
    14	const { activeChanges, parseFlowState } = require('./status');
    15	const { containsReal } = require('./archive-merge');
    16	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    17	const { legacyRoots } = require('./resolve');
    18	
    19	const NODE_FLOOR = 22;
    20	
    21	// type-honest probes: doctor's whole job is malformed scaffolds — a file impersonating a
    22	// directory (or vice versa) must become a FINDING, never a false pass or a crash
    23	function isDir(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isDirectory(); } catch { return false; } }
    24	function isFile(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isFile(); } catch { return false; } }
    25	function readIfFile(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isFile() ? fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8') : null; } catch { return null; } }
    26	
    27	// D5's classifier over a runTestCommand result — exported for the Windows-safe signal test (DR-06)
    28	function classifyProbe(run) {
    29	  // run = runTestCommand's shape: { out, status, signal, error }
    30	  const { out } = run;
    31	  if (run.error) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command failed to spawn: ${run.error}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    32	  if (run.signal) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command was killed by signal ${run.signal}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    33	  const idRe = new RegExp(DEFAULT_ID);
    34	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, tapProblems } = parseTap(out, idRe);
    35	  if (bailout) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test run aborted: ${bailout}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    36	  // the lexer's protocol problems (unsupported version, YAML, plan placement) are seam findings (DR-14)
    37	  if (tapProblems && tapProblems.length)
    38	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `TAP protocol: ${tapProblems[0]}`, fix: 'emit supported TAP (12/13/14 or no version line) from the test command' };
    39	  // TAP on stderr with a silent stdout: the channel is misrouted, not missing (DR-14)
    40	  if (!out.trim() && looksLikeTap(run.stderr))
    41	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'TAP arrives on stderr while stdout is silent — verify reads stdout only', fix: 'append 2>&1 to the test command' };
    42	  if (!out.trim()) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'test command produced no output', fix: 'fix the test command' };
    43	  if (zeroTapParsed(out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures))
    44	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'output is not TAP — node test runner needs --test-reporter=tap', fix: 'emit TAP from the test command' };
    45	  const parsed = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0) + untagged.length + unattributedFailures.length;
    46	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
    47	  // an unexplained non-zero exit is classified BEFORE any ok branch — a non-zero `1..0` run lands here
    48	  if (run.status !== null && run.status !== 0 && failCount === 0)
    49	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command exited ${run.status} unexplained by TAP — truncated?`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    50	  if (parsed === 0) {
    51	    if (/^1\.\.0\b/m.test(out)) return { id: 'D5', status: 'ok', detail: 'TAP plumbing OK (empty suite, 1..0)' };
    52	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'TAP stream truncated or malformed (version/plan but zero result lines)', fix: 'fix the test command' };
    53	  }
    54	  const pass = parsed - failCount;
    55	  return { id: 'D5', status: 'ok', detail: `TAP plumbing OK (${pass}p/${failCount}f — test failures are verify's business, not doctor's)` };
    56	}
    57	
    58	// The whole diagnosis. → { code, result, findings, checks, errors }
    59	function runDoctor(opts = {}) {
    60	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
    61	  const checks = [], errors = [];
    62	  const finish = (code) => {
    63	    const findings = checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'finding').length;
    64	    const result = code === 2 ? 'UNUSABLE' : (findings ? 'FINDINGS' : 'HEALTHY');
    65	    return { code: code !== undefined ? code : (findings ? 1 : 0), result, findings, checks, errors };
    66	  };
    67	
    68	  // D1 — Node floor (version injectable for tests, DR-12)
    69	  const major = parseInt((opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node), 10);
    70	  if (major < NODE_FLOOR) {
    71	    checks.push({ id: 'D1', status: 'finding', detail: `Node ${opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node} is below the supported floor (>= ${NODE_FLOOR})`, fix: 'upgrade Node' });
    72	    errors.push(`Node below the supported floor (>= ${NODE_FLOOR})`);
    73	    return finish(2);
    74	  }
    75	  checks.push({ id: 'D1', status: 'ok', detail: `Node ${opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node} >= ${NODE_FLOOR}` });

    80	    checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'finding', detail: `no apriori/ here — not an apriori project yet`, fix: `run 'apriori init'` });
    81	    errors.push(`no apriori/ directory — run 'apriori init'`);
    82	    return finish(2);
    83	  }
    84	  const gaps = [];
    85	  if (!isFile(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md'))) gaps.push([`apriori/runbook.md ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md')) ? 'is not a regular file' : 'missing'}`, `apriori init`]);
    86	  if (!isDir(path.join(ap, 'specs'))) gaps.push([`apriori/specs/ ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'specs')) ? 'is not a directory' : 'missing (half-initialized scaffold)'}`, `apriori init`]);
    87	  const giText = readIfFile(path.join(ap, '.gitignore'));
    88	  if (giText === null || !/^tmp\/$/m.test(giText)) gaps.push(['apriori/.gitignore missing, unreadable, or missing its tmp/ line', `apriori update`]);
    89	  if (!isDir(path.join(ap, 'tmp'))) gaps.push([`apriori/tmp/ ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'tmp')) ? 'is not a directory' : 'dir missing'}`, `apriori update / mkdir`]);
    90	  if (gaps.length) for (const [detail, fix] of gaps) checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'finding', detail, fix });

    92

    98	    checks.push(warn.length
    99	      ? { id: 'D3', status: 'finding', detail: warn[0], fix: 'apriori update' }
   100	      : { id: 'D3', status: 'ok', detail: 'runbook copy matches the installed CLI' });
   101	  }

   103	  // D4 — tool pointers for DETECTED tools only

   106	  else {
   107	    let broken = 0;
   108	    for (const key of detected) {

   114	        broken++;
   115	      }

   118	        broken++;
   119	      }

   151	    }
